import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// ?舀???圈?隤??游???enum AppLanguage {
  zhTW(
    code: 'zh-TW',
    shortLabel: '蝜葉',
    fullLabel: '蝜?銝剜? (?啁)',
    flagEmoji: '??',
  ),
  enUS(
    code: 'en-US',
    shortLabel: 'EN',
    fullLabel: 'English (US)',
    flagEmoji: '??',
  );

  const AppLanguage({
    required this.code,
    required this.shortLabel,
    required this.fullLabel,
    required this.flagEmoji,
  });

  final String code;
  final String shortLabel;
  final String fullLabel;
  final String flagEmoji;

  bool get isZh => this == AppLanguage.zhTW;
  bool get isEn => this == AppLanguage.enUS;
}

/// 擃??桐? (?啁?身 kg嚗???閮?lbs)
enum WeightUnit {
  kg('kg'),
  lbs('lbs');

  const WeightUnit(this.symbol);
  final String symbol;
}

/// 憌脫偌?雿?(?啁?身 ml嚗???閮?oz)
enum WaterUnit {
  ml('ml'),
  oz('oz');

  const WaterUnit(this.symbol);
  final String symbol;
}

/// ?典?銝剛?????啣??渡???嗅
class LocaleController extends ChangeNotifier {
  LocaleController({
    AppLanguage initialLanguage = AppLanguage.zhTW,
  })  : _language = initialLanguage,
        _weightUnit =
            initialLanguage.isZh ? WeightUnit.kg : WeightUnit.lbs,
        _waterUnit =
            initialLanguage.isZh ? WaterUnit.ml : WaterUnit.oz;

  AppLanguage _language;
  WeightUnit _weightUnit;
  WaterUnit _waterUnit;

  AppLanguage get language => _language;
  WeightUnit get weightUnit => _weightUnit;
  WaterUnit get waterUnit => _waterUnit;
  bool get isZh => _language.isZh;

  /// 銝?萄??葉?望?嚗蒂?箸???蝢?身摨阡?銵∪雿?
  void toggleLanguage() {
    setLanguage(_language.isZh ? AppLanguage.enUS : AppLanguage.zhTW);
  }

  /// ???格?隤頂
  void setLanguage(AppLanguage newLanguage, {bool syncUnits = true}) {
    if (_language == newLanguage) return;
    _language = newLanguage;
    if (syncUnits) {
      _weightUnit = newLanguage.isZh ? WeightUnit.kg : WeightUnit.lbs;
      _waterUnit = newLanguage.isZh ? WaterUnit.ml : WaterUnit.oz;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// ????擃??桐? (kg <-> lbs)
  void setWeightUnit(WeightUnit unit) {
    if (_weightUnit == unit) return;
    _weightUnit = unit;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// ????憌脫偌?桐? (ml <-> oz)
  void setWaterUnit(WaterUnit unit) {
    if (_waterUnit == unit) return;
    _waterUnit = unit;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// ?寞??嗅?隤頂?撠?摮葡
  String tr(String zhText, String enText) {
    return _language.isZh ? zhText : enText;
  }
}

/// ?典?????釣?亙 (InheritedNotifier ?嗆?嚗???蝟餅??芸??鼓摮邦)
class AppLocaleScope extends InheritedNotifier<LocaleController> {
  const AppLocaleScope({
    super.key,
    required LocaleController controller,
    required super.child,
  }) : super(notifier: controller);

  static LocaleController of(BuildContext context) {
    final AppLocaleScope? scope =
        context.dependOnInheritedWidgetOfExactType<AppLocaleScope>();
    assert(scope != null, 'No AppLocaleScope found in widget tree.');
    return scope!.notifier!;
  }

  static AppStrings stringsOf(BuildContext context) {
    return AppStrings(of(context).language);
  }
}

/// BuildContext 靘踵?游?嚗?隞颱? Widget ?舐?亙??context.strings ??context.locale
extension AppLocalizationExtension on BuildContext {
  LocaleController get locale => AppLocaleScope.of(this);
  AppStrings get strings => AppLocaleScope.stringsOf(this);
  bool get isZh => locale.isZh;
}

/// ?撠汗???剁?銝?萎葉?勗???????(?? 蝜葉 <-> ?? EN)
class LanguageTogglePill extends StatelessWidget {
  const LanguageTogglePill({
    super.key,
    this.compact = false,
  });

  final bool compact;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final LocaleController controller = context.locale;
    final bool isZh = controller.isZh;

    return Semantics(
      button: true,
      label: isZh ? '???箄??(Switch to English)' : 'Switch to Traditional Chinese (???箇?擃葉??',
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
          onTap: controller.toggleLanguage,
          child: AnimatedContainer(
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
            curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: compact ? 8.0 : 10.0,
              vertical: 5.0,
            ),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
              border: Border.all(
                color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.38),
                width: 1.2,
              ),
            ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: <Widget>[
                _buildSegment(
                  label: '?? 蝜葉',
                  active: isZh,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                _buildSegment(
                  label: '?? EN',
                  active: !isZh,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSegment({required String label, required bool active}) {
    return AnimatedContainer(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7.0, vertical: 3.0),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: active
            ? const Color(0xFF0EA5E9)
            : Colors.transparent,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
      ),
      child: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 11.5,
          fontWeight: active ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
          color: active ? Colors.white : const Color(0xFF334155),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 蝢摨阡?銵∪翰??? (kg <-> lbs / ml <-> oz)
class UnitSwitchBar extends StatelessWidget {
  const UnitSwitchBar({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final LocaleController controller = context.locale;
    final bool isZh = controller.isZh;

    return Wrap(
      spacing: 8.0,
      runSpacing: 8.0,
      crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
      children: <Widget>[
        ChoiceChip(
          label: Text(isZh ? '擃?: kg (?祆)' : 'Weight: kg'),
          selected: controller.weightUnit == WeightUnit.kg,
          onSelected: (_) => controller.setWeightUnit(WeightUnit.kg),
        ),
        ChoiceChip(
          label: Text(isZh ? '擃?: lbs (蝤?' : 'Weight: lbs'),
          selected: controller.weightUnit == WeightUnit.lbs,
          onSelected: (_) => controller.setWeightUnit(WeightUnit.lbs),
        ),
        ChoiceChip(
          label: Text(isZh ? '憌脫偌: ml (瘥怠?)' : 'Water: ml'),
          selected: controller.waterUnit == WaterUnit.ml,
          onSelected: (_) => controller.setWaterUnit(WeightUnit.kg == controller.weightUnit ? WaterUnit.ml : WaterUnit.ml),
        ),
        ChoiceChip(
          label: Text(isZh ? '憌脫偌: oz (?)' : 'Water: oz'),
          selected: controller.waterUnit == WaterUnit.oz,
          onSelected: (_) => controller.setWaterUnit(WaterUnit.oz),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


=== lib/core/app_persistence.dart ===

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';

/// ?蝔????翰?扯??芋??撠????閬蝺摮?雿輻?摨??
@immutable
class AppStorageSnapshot {
  const AppStorageSnapshot({
    required this.logs,
    required this.dailyProteinGrams,
    required this.targetProteinGrams,
    required this.dailyWaterMl,
    required this.targetWaterMl,
    required this.bowelCompletedToday,
    required this.startWeightKg,
    required this.targetWeightKg,
    required this.lastSavedTimestamp,
    this.schemaVersion = 1,
  });

  final List<InjectionRecord> logs;
  final int dailyProteinGrams;
  final int targetProteinGrams;
  final int dailyWaterMl;
  final int targetWaterMl;
  final bool bowelCompletedToday;
  final double startWeightKg;
  final double targetWeightKg;
  final DateTime lastSavedTimestamp;
  final int schemaVersion;

  /// ?身???典??豢?敹怎
  factory AppStorageSnapshot.initial() {
    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    return AppStorageSnapshot(
      logs: <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'init_shot_1',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 2, hours: 4)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
          painLevel: 1,
        ),
      ],
      dailyProteinGrams: 45,
      targetProteinGrams: 90,
      dailyWaterMl: 1250,
      targetWaterMl: 2500,
      bowelCompletedToday: false,
      startWeightKg: 85.0,
      targetWeightKg: 70.0,
      lastSavedTimestamp: now,
      schemaVersion: 1,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return <String, dynamic>{
      'schemaVersion': schemaVersion,
      'lastSavedTimestamp': lastSavedTimestamp.toIso8601String(),
      'dailyProteinGrams': dailyProteinGrams,
      'targetProteinGrams': targetProteinGrams,
      'dailyWaterMl': dailyWaterMl,
      'targetWaterMl': targetWaterMl,
      'bowelCompletedToday': bowelCompletedToday,
      'startWeightKg': startWeightKg,
      'targetWeightKg': targetWeightKg,
      'logs': logs.map((InjectionRecord r) {
        return <String, dynamic>{
          'id': r.id,
          'timestamp': r.timestamp.toIso8601String(),
          'medicationId': r.medication.id,
          'doseMg': r.doseMg,
          'siteName': r.site.name,
          'painLevel': r.painLevel,
          'batchOrVialNote': r.batchOrVialNote,
        };
      }).toList(),
    };
  }

  factory AppStorageSnapshot.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    final List<dynamic>? rawLogs = map['logs'] as List<dynamic>?;
    final List<InjectionRecord> parsedLogs = <InjectionRecord>[];

    if (rawLogs != null) {
      for (final dynamic item in rawLogs) {
        if (item is Map<String, dynamic>) {
          final String? medId = item['medicationId'] as String?;
          final Glp1Medication med = Glp1Medication.values.firstWhere(
            (Glp1Medication m) => m.id == medId,
            orElse: () => Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          );
          final String? siteName = item['siteName'] as String?;
          final InjectionSite site = InjectionSite.values.firstWhere(
            (InjectionSite s) => s.name == siteName,
            orElse: () => InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
          );

          parsedLogs.add(
            InjectionRecord(
              id: item['id'] as String? ?? 'shot_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
              timestamp: item['timestamp'] != null
                  ? (DateTime.tryParse(item['timestamp'] as String) ?? DateTime.now())
                  : DateTime.now(),
              medication: med,
              doseMg: (item['doseMg'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 2.5,
              site: site,
              painLevel: item['painLevel'] as int? ?? 0,
              batchOrVialNote: item['batchOrVialNote'] as String?,
            ),
          );
        }
      }
    }

    return AppStorageSnapshot(
      schemaVersion: map['schemaVersion'] as int? ?? 1,
      lastSavedTimestamp: map['lastSavedTimestamp'] != null
          ? (DateTime.tryParse(map['lastSavedTimestamp'] as String) ?? DateTime.now())
          : DateTime.now(),
      dailyProteinGrams: map['dailyProteinGrams'] as int? ?? 0,
      targetProteinGrams: map['targetProteinGrams'] as int? ?? 90,
      dailyWaterMl: map['dailyWaterMl'] as int? ?? 0,
      targetWaterMl: map['targetWaterMl'] as int? ?? 2500,
      bowelCompletedToday: map['bowelCompletedToday'] as bool? ?? false,
      startWeightKg: (map['startWeightKg'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 85.0,
      targetWeightKg: (map['targetWeightKg'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 70.0,
      logs: parsedLogs.isNotEmpty ? parsedLogs : AppStorageSnapshot.initial().logs,
    );
  }
}

/// ?詨??砍???恣????100% Local-First嚗隡箸??剁??怎??梁??擃?蝝?
class AppPersistence {
  AppPersistence._();

  static final AppPersistence instance = AppPersistence._();

  static const String storageFileName = 'dose_flow_storage_v1.json';

  /// ?嗅?閮擃翰?批翰????敺虜擏??園?嚗?  AppStorageSnapshot _cachedSnapshot = AppStorageSnapshot.initial();
  AppStorageSnapshot get current => _cachedSnapshot;

  /// ?之?脤?萄? 06嚗葫閰行??? Web ?啣??拍?蝯楠靽風??  static bool get _isIsolatedSandbox {
    if (kIsWeb) return true;
    try {
      if (Platform.environment.containsKey('FLUTTER_TEST')) return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /// ?閮擃摮嚗?皜祈岫瘝???Web 蝡臭蝙?剁?
  static String? _memoryStorageContent;

  /// ??撖阡?摮?瑼?嚗??憭??芾?摰??嚗?  File? _getStorageFile() {
    if (_isIsolatedSandbox) return null;
    try {
      return File(storageFileName);
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// 頛?祆?摮?敹怎嚗銝??冽???????閮剖潘?
  Future<AppStorageSnapshot> loadSnapshot() async {
    // 1. 瘝??啣?韏啗??園??瑼?蝟餌絞
    if (_isIsolatedSandbox) {
      if (_memoryStorageContent != null) {
        try {
          final Map<String, dynamic> map =
              jsonDecode(_memoryStorageContent!) as Map<String, dynamic>;
          _cachedSnapshot = AppStorageSnapshot.fromMap(map);
          return _cachedSnapshot;
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      _cachedSnapshot = AppStorageSnapshot.initial();
      return _cachedSnapshot;
    }

    // 2. 撖阡?蝤?頛
    try {
      final File? file = _getStorageFile();
      if (file != null && await file.exists()) {
        final String rawJson = await file.readAsString();
        final String cleanJson = rawJson.replaceAll('\uFEFF', '').trim();
        if (cleanJson.isNotEmpty) {
          final Map<String, dynamic> map =
              jsonDecode(cleanJson) as Map<String, dynamic>;
          _cachedSnapshot = AppStorageSnapshot.fromMap(map);
          return _cachedSnapshot;
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    _cachedSnapshot = AppStorageSnapshot.initial();
    return _cachedSnapshot;
  }

  /// ?脣?敹怎?單璈??∠???神?仿甇Ｘ?餅?撏拇蔑??摮???嚗?  Future<bool> saveSnapshot(AppStorageSnapshot snapshot) async {
    _cachedSnapshot = snapshot;

    final String jsonString = const JsonEncoder.withIndent('  ')
        .convert(snapshot.toMap());

    // 1. 瘝??啣?撖怠閮擃?    if (_isIsolatedSandbox) {
      _memoryStorageContent = jsonString;
      return true;
    }

    // 2. 撖阡?蝤????神??(?神??.tmp 瑼?????
    try {
      final File? targetFile = _getStorageFile();
      if (targetFile != null) {
        final File tmpFile = File('${targetFile.path}.tmp');
        await tmpFile.writeAsString(jsonString, flush: true);
        if (await tmpFile.exists()) {
          await tmpFile.rename(targetFile.path);
          return true;
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      _memoryStorageContent = jsonString;
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  /// ?之?脤?萄? 09嚗ase64 ?湔摮?甇?????詨捆瑼Ｘ??  static bool isValidBackupBase64(String? raw) {
    if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty) return false;
    final String clean =
        raw.replaceAll('\r', '').replaceAll('\n', '').trim();
    if (clean.length % 4 != 0) return false;
    return RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$').hasMatch(clean);
  }

  /// 銝?萄?箸璈??隞賢?銝莎?Base64 ?脩砥?孵?蝣澆飛摮葡嚗?  String exportBackupString() {
    final String jsonString = jsonEncode(_cachedSnapshot.toMap());
    return base64Encode(utf8.encode(jsonString));
  }

  /// 銝?萄?亙?隞賢?銝莎??瑕??脤?萄? 09 ?湔瑼ａ??????瑼?
  Future<bool> importBackupString(String backupString) async {
    if (!isValidBackupBase64(backupString)) return false;

    try {
      final String clean =
          backupString.replaceAll('\r', '').replaceAll('\n', '').trim();
      final String decodedJson = utf8.decode(base64Decode(clean));
      final Map<String, dynamic> map =
          jsonDecode(decodedJson) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final AppStorageSnapshot imported = AppStorageSnapshot.fromMap(map);
      await saveSnapshot(imported);
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// ?Ｗ儔???身?潘?皜征摮?皜祈岫?剁?
  Future<void> resetToInitial() async {
    await saveSnapshot(AppStorageSnapshot.initial());
  }
}


=== lib/core/clinical_export_service.dart ===

import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'app_localization.dart';
import 'pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';

/// ?臬?梯”?澆???
enum ClinicalExportFormat {
  csv('CSV 銵冽 (Excel/Numbers ?詨捆)'),
  physicianHtml('銝???怠葦?那?? (HTML/???拙?)'),
  plainText('蝝?摮?甇瑟?閬?(?舐?亥?鋆?');

  const ClinicalExportFormat(this.label);
  final String label;
}

/// ?典??怎?皞?芋??靘??憚?輻?????嚗?@immutable
class ClinicalAdherenceMetrics {
  const ClinicalAdherenceMetrics({
    required this.adherenceRatePercent,
    required this.siteRotationCompliancePercent,
    required this.weeklyWeightVelocityKg,
    required this.totalLossKg,
    required this.totalLogsCount,
    required this.distinctSitesUsed,
  });

  final double adherenceRatePercent;
  final double siteRotationCompliancePercent;
  final double weeklyWeightVelocityKg;
  final double totalLossKg;
  final int totalLogsCount;
  final int distinctSitesUsed;
}

/// 蝯??摨銵刻? CSV ?臬撘?嚗敺?RFC 4180 璅?嚗?class ClinicalExportService {
  const ClinicalExportService._();

  /// 瞍?瘜?閮? 30 憭拇??敺??雿憚?輸敺????望???
  static ClinicalAdherenceMetrics calculateMetrics({
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required double startWeightKg,
    required double currentWeightKg,
  }) {
    if (logs.isEmpty) {
      return const ClinicalAdherenceMetrics(
        adherenceRatePercent: 100.0,
        siteRotationCompliancePercent: 100.0,
        weeklyWeightVelocityKg: 0.0,
        totalLossKg: 0.0,
        totalLogsCount: 0,
        distinctSitesUsed: 0,
      );
    }

    // 靘????唳?摨?    final List<InjectionRecord> sorted = List<InjectionRecord>.from(logs)
      ..sort((InjectionRecord a, InjectionRecord b) =>
          a.timestamp.compareTo(b.timestamp));

    // 1. 閮??其?頛芣??(?賊?拇活?賣??其?銝?閬?萄?頛芣)
    int compliantRotations = 0;
    final Set<InjectionSite> sitesUsed = <InjectionSite>{};
    for (int i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
      sitesUsed.add(sorted[i].site);
      if (i > 0) {
        if (sorted[i].site != sorted[i - 1].site) {
          compliantRotations++;
        }
      }
    }
    final double rotationRate = sorted.length > 1
        ? (compliantRotations / (sorted.length - 1)) * 100.0
        : 100.0;

    // 2. 閮??賣?靘???(?賊?拇活????6 ~ 8 憭抵??箸????
    int onScheduleShots = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
      final double days =
          sorted[i].timestamp.difference(sorted[i - 1].timestamp).inHours /
              24.0;
      if (days >= 5.8 && days <= 8.5) {
        onScheduleShots++;
      }
    }
    final double adherenceRate = sorted.length > 1
        ? (onScheduleShots / (sorted.length - 1)) * 100.0
        : 100.0;

    // 3. 閮?蝝航?皜????望???    final double totalLoss = (startWeightKg - currentWeightKg).clamp(0.0, 100.0);
    final double totalDays = math.max(
      1.0,
      DateTime.now().difference(sorted.first.timestamp).inHours / 24.0,
    );
    final double weeks = totalDays / 7.0;
    final double velocity = weeks > 0 ? (totalLoss / weeks) : 0.0;

    return ClinicalAdherenceMetrics(
      adherenceRatePercent:
          double.parse(adherenceRate.clamp(0.0, 100.0).toStringAsFixed(1)),
      siteRotationCompliancePercent:
          double.parse(rotationRate.clamp(0.0, 100.0).toStringAsFixed(1)),
      weeklyWeightVelocityKg: double.parse(velocity.toStringAsFixed(2)),
      totalLossKg: double.parse(totalLoss.toStringAsFixed(1)),
      totalLogsCount: logs.length,
      distinctSitesUsed: sitesUsed.length,
    );
  }

  /// ?Ｙ?璅? RFC 4180 CSV ?澆?銋??風?脫??  static String generateCsv({
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required AppLanguage lang,
  }) {
    final StringBuffer buffer = StringBuffer();

    // CSV 銵券
    if (lang.isZh) {
      buffer.writeln(
        '?賣?摨?,?交?,??,?亦??,?賣???(mg),瘜典??其?,?潛???0-5),?酉/?亦?寡?',
      );
    } else {
      buffer.writeln(
        'ShotIndex,Date,Time,Medication,Dose(mg),InjectionSite,PainLevel(0-5),BatchOrNotes',
      );
    }

    final List<InjectionRecord> sorted = List<InjectionRecord>.from(logs)
      ..sort((InjectionRecord a, InjectionRecord b) =>
          a.timestamp.compareTo(b.timestamp));

    for (int i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
      final InjectionRecord r = sorted[i];
      final String dateStr =
          '${r.timestamp.year}-${r.timestamp.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${r.timestamp.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
      final String timeStr =
          '${r.timestamp.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${r.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
      final String medName = _escapeCsvField(r.medication.displayName(lang));
      final String siteName = _escapeCsvField(r.site.label(lang));
      final String note = _escapeCsvField(r.batchOrVialNote ?? '');

      buffer.writeln(
        '#${i + 1},$dateStr,$timeStr,$medName,${r.doseMg.toStringAsFixed(2)},$siteName,${r.painLevel},$note',
      );
    }

    return buffer.toString();
  }

  /// RFC 4180 甈?頝唾嚗??恍???銵?撘???誑????鋆嫣蒂頧儔
  static String _escapeCsvField(String value) {
    if (value.contains(',') || value.contains('"') || value.contains('\n')) {
      return '"${value.replaceAll('"', '""')}"';
    }
    return value;
  }

  /// ?Ｙ????拙?銋????怠葦?那?典??梯” HTML (?芸葆? CSS ?撣急?行筑瘞游)
  static String generatePhysicianSummaryHtml({
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required double startWeightKg,
    required double currentWeightKg,
    required AppLanguage lang,
    required WeightUnit unit,
  }) {
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;
    final ClinicalAdherenceMetrics m = calculateMetrics(
      logs: logs,
      startWeightKg: startWeightKg,
      currentWeightKg: currentWeightKg,
    );

    final double displayStart =
        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (startWeightKg * 2.20462) : startWeightKg;
    final double displayCurrent =
        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (currentWeightKg * 2.20462) : currentWeightKg;
    final double displayLoss =
        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (m.totalLossKg * 2.20462) : m.totalLossKg;

    final String primaryMedName = logs.isNotEmpty
        ? logs.first.medication.displayName(lang)
        : 'Tirzepatide';

    // ???餈?6 蝑????銵冽皜脫?
    final List<InjectionRecord> recentLogs = List<InjectionRecord>.from(logs)
      ..sort((InjectionRecord a, InjectionRecord b) =>
          b.timestamp.compareTo(a.timestamp));
    final List<InjectionRecord> displayRecent =
        recentLogs.take(6).toList();

    final StringBuffer tableRows = StringBuffer();
    for (int i = 0; i < displayRecent.length; i++) {
      final InjectionRecord r = displayRecent[i];
      final String dateStr =
          '${r.timestamp.year}-${r.timestamp.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${r.timestamp.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
      tableRows.writeln('''
      <tr>
        <td>#${logs.length - i}</td>
        <td>$dateStr</td>
        <td><strong>${r.doseMg.toStringAsFixed(1)} mg</strong></td>
        <td>${r.site.label(lang)}</td>

    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required double startWeightKg,
    required double currentWeightKg,
    required AppLanguage lang,
    required WeightUnit unit,
  }) {

      logs: logs,

    );

        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (startWeightKg * 2.20462) : startWeightKg;
    final double displayCurrent =
        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (currentWeightKg * 2.20462) : currentWeightKg;
    final double displayLoss =
        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (m.totalLossKg * 2.20462) : m.totalLossKg;

    final String medName = logs.isNotEmpty
        ? logs.first.medication.displayName(lang)
        : 'Tirzepatide';

??蝝航?蝝??撌脰???${logs.length} ??蝔?????嚗??勗像????~${(m.weeklyWeightVelocityKg * (unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? 2.20462 : 1.0)).toStringAsFixed(2)} ${unit.symbol}嚗??湧??貉?銝??(?祆?閬 DoseFlow ?芸???嚗摨瑟??100% ?脣??潛???璈?蝘犖 iCloud)''';
    } else {
      return '''
[DoseFlow Clinical Visit Summary]
??Medication: $medName
??Baseline Weight: ${displayStart.toStringAsFixed(1)} ${unit.symbol}
??Current Weight: ${displayCurrent.toStringAsFixed(1)} ${unit.symbol} (Total Loss: -${displayLoss.toStringAsFixed(1)} ${unit.symbol})

  /// 銝?萄??券??賣?蝝??CSV 撖阡?撖怠?祆?撠??桅? (?舀 Excel/Numbers)
  static Future<String?> exportCsvToDisk({
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required AppLanguage lang,
  }) async {
    if (kIsWeb) return null;
    try {
      final String csv = generateCsv(logs: logs, lang: lang);
      final File file = File('dose_flow_clinical_history.csv');
      await file.writeAsString(csv, flush: true);
      return file.path;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// 銝?萄??怠葦?那銝?? HTML ?梯”撖阡?撖怠?祆??桅? (?舐?仿?????
  static Future<String?> exportSummaryHtmlToDisk({
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required double startWeightKg,
    required double currentWeightKg,
    required AppLanguage lang,
    required WeightUnit unit,
  }) async {
    if (kIsWeb) return null;
    try {
      final String html = generatePhysicianSummaryHtml(
        logs: logs,
        startWeightKg: startWeightKg,
        currentWeightKg: currentWeightKg,
        lang: lang,
        unit: unit,
      );
      final File file = File('dose_flow_physician_summary.html');
      await file.writeAsString(html, flush: true);
      return file.path;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

/// ?典??豢??臬?撣怠?閮箏??閬賢?閰望?
class ClinicalExportDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const ClinicalExportDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.logs,
    required this.startWeightKg,
    required this.currentWeightKg,
    this.initialFormat = ClinicalExportFormat.physicianHtml,
  });

  final List<InjectionRecord> logs;
  final double startWeightKg;
  final double currentWeightKg;
  final ClinicalExportFormat initialFormat;

  @override
  State<ClinicalExportDialog> createState() => _ClinicalExportDialogState();
}

class _ClinicalExportDialogState extends State<ClinicalExportDialog> {
  late ClinicalExportFormat _selectedFormat;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _selectedFormat = widget.initialFormat;
  }

  String _getPreviewContent(AppLanguage lang, WeightUnit unit) {
    switch (_selectedFormat) {
      case ClinicalExportFormat.physicianHtml:
        return ClinicalExportService.generatePhysicianSummaryHtml(
          logs: widget.logs,
          startWeightKg: widget.startWeightKg,
          currentWeightKg: widget.currentWeightKg,
          lang: lang,
          unit: unit,
        );
      case ClinicalExportFormat.plainText:
        return ClinicalExportService.generatePlainTextSummary(
          logs: widget.logs,
          startWeightKg: widget.startWeightKg,
          currentWeightKg: widget.currentWeightKg,
          lang: lang,
          unit: unit,
        );
      case ClinicalExportFormat.csv:
        return ClinicalExportService.generateCsv(
          logs: widget.logs,
          lang: lang,
        );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppLanguage lang = context.locale.language;
    final WeightUnit unit = context.locale.weightUnit;
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;
    final String contentText = _getPreviewContent(lang, unit);

    return AlertDialog(
      title: Row(
        children: <Widget>[
          const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf_rounded, color: Color(0xFF0EA5E9), size: 24),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              isZh ? '?那?怎??豢??臬銝剜?' : 'Clinical Visit Data Export',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 620,
        height: 480,
        child: Column(
          children: <Widget>[
            // ?澆???璅惜 (HTML ?梯” / 蝝?摮?甇?/ CSV)
            SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              child: Row(
                children: ClinicalExportFormat.values.map((ClinicalExportFormat fmt) {
                  final bool isSelected = fmt == _selectedFormat;
                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6.0),
                    child: ChoiceChip(
                      label: Text(
                        fmt.label,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
                        ),
                      ),
                      selected: isSelected,
                      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _selectedFormat = fmt),
                    ),
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            // ???批捆?汗?
            Expanded(
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFCBD5E1)),
                ),
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  child: SelectableText(
                    contentText,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 11.0,
                      fontFamily: 'Consolas',
                      height: 1.45,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: <Widget>[
        // ?臬撖阡?瑼???
        TextButton.icon(
          onPressed: () async {
            String? path;
            if (_selectedFormat == ClinicalExportFormat.csv) {
              path = await ClinicalExportService.exportCsvToDisk(
                logs: widget.logs,
                lang: lang,
              );
            } else {
              path = await ClinicalExportService.exportSummaryHtmlToDisk(
                logs: widget.logs,
                startWeightKg: widget.startWeightKg,
                currentWeightKg: widget.currentWeightKg,
                lang: lang,
                unit: unit,
              );
            }

            if (context.mounted) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(
                    path != null
                        ? (isZh
                            ? '?? 撌脣?祆?撠??寧????獢?$path嚗?
                            : '?? Exported file to root: $path!')
                        : (isZh
                            ? '?? 撖怠憭望?????Web ?啣?'
                            : '?? Export restricted in web environment'),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }
          },
          icon: const Icon(Icons.file_download_outlined, size: 16),
          label: Text(isZh ? '?臬撖阡?瑼??單璈? : 'Export File to Disk'),
        ),
        // 銴ˊ蝝?摮???        TextButton.icon(
          onPressed: () {
            Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: contentText));
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(
                  isZh
                      ? '?? 撌脰?鋆賢摰寡?芾票蝪選??舐?亥票蝯阡撣怒?
                      : '?? Copied content to clipboard!',
                ),
              ),
            );
          },
          icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 16),
          label: Text(isZh ? '銴ˊ?批捆' : 'Copy Text'),
        ),
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: Text(isZh ? '??' : 'Close'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'app_localization.dart';
import 'haptic_feedback_service.dart';
import 'pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';

/// ???臭??函雁摨行???enum SideEffectType {
  nausea('????', 'Nausea'),
  fatigue('?刻澈?脣?', 'Fatigue'),
  acidReflux('????/?寞除', 'Acid Reflux / Bloating'),
  constipation('?噶銝?/靘輻?', 'Constipation');

  const SideEffectType(this.labelZh, this.labelEn);
  final String labelZh;
  final String labelEn;

  String label(AppLanguage lang) => lang.isZh ? labelZh : labelEn;
}

/// 瘥憌??雿銝餉?蝝?芋??(PRD 蝚?2.4 蝭璅?璅∪?)
@immutable
class DailySideEffectLog {
  const DailySideEffectLog({
    required this.id,
    required this.date,
    required this.foodNoiseSuppression,
    this.nauseaLevel = 0,
    this.fatigueLevel = 0,
    this.acidRefluxLevel = 0,
    this.constipationLevel = 0,
    this.notes,
  });

  final String id;
  final DateTime date;

  /// 憌??銝剜?镼輸??喳??嗥?摨佗?
  /// 5 = 摰摰? (Optimal / Complete Silence)
  /// 4 = 憿航?? (Strongly Suppressed)
  /// 3 = 銝剖漲? (Moderate Control)
  /// 2 = ?敺格筑??(Mild Food Noise Creeping Back)
  /// 1 = 憌?券?/?撌典之 (Full Food Noise / Breakthrough Hunger)
  final int foodNoiseSuppression;

  /// ???臭??函?蝝?(0 = ?? 1 = 頛凝, 3 = 銝剖漲, 5 = ?湧?)
  final int nauseaLevel;
  final int fatigueLevel;
  final int acidRefluxLevel;
  final int constipationLevel;
  final String? notes;

  /// 蝬??臭??典?漲閰? (0 ~ 5)
  double get overallSymptomSeverity {
    final int maxLevel =
        math.max(math.max(nauseaLevel, fatigueLevel), math.max(acidRefluxLevel, constipationLevel));
    final double avg = (nauseaLevel + fatigueLevel + acidRefluxLevel + constipationLevel) / 4.0;
    return double.parse(((maxLevel * 0.7) + (avg * 0.3)).clamp(0.0, 5.0).toStringAsFixed(1));
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return <String, dynamic>{
      'id': id,
      'date': date.toIso8601String(),
      'foodNoiseSuppression': foodNoiseSuppression,
      'nauseaLevel': nauseaLevel,
      'fatigueLevel': fatigueLevel,
      'acidRefluxLevel': acidRefluxLevel,
      'constipationLevel': constipationLevel,
      'notes': notes,
    };
  }

  factory DailySideEffectLog.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return DailySideEffectLog(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? 'se_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      date: map['date'] != null
          ? (DateTime.tryParse(map['date'] as String) ?? DateTime.now())
          : DateTime.now(),
      foodNoiseSuppression:
          ((map['foodNoiseSuppression'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 5).clamp(1, 5),
      nauseaLevel: ((map['nauseaLevel'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0).clamp(0, 5),
      fatigueLevel: ((map['fatigueLevel'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0).clamp(0, 5),
      acidRefluxLevel:
          ((map['acidRefluxLevel'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0).clamp(0, 5),
      constipationLevel:
          ((map['constipationLevel'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0).clamp(0, 5),
      notes: map['notes'] as String?,
    );
  }
}

/// ?賣?敺洵 1~7 憭拚??舀???Ｘ芋??@immutable
class DayCorrelationProfile {
  const DayCorrelationProfile({
    required this.postShotDay,
    required this.avgFoodNoiseSuppression,
    required this.avgNausea,
    required this.avgFatigue,
    required this.avgAcidReflux,
    required this.avgConstipation,
    required this.sampleCount,
  });

  final int postShotDay; // 1 ~ 7
  final double avgFoodNoiseSuppression; // 1.0 ~ 5.0 (5=憯?撘??摰?)
  final double avgNausea; // 0.0 ~ 5.0
  final double avgFatigue; // 0.0 ~ 5.0
  final double avgAcidReflux; // 0.0 ~ 5.0
  final double avgConstipation; // 0.0 ~ 5.0
  final int sampleCount;
}

/// ??芣?????撱箄降璅∪?
@immutable
class InjectionDayRecommendation {
  const InjectionDayRecommendation({
    required this.recommendedWeekday,
    required this.recommendedTimeLabel,
    required this.peakSymptomDayOffset,
    required this.rationaleZh,
    required this.rationaleEn,
  });

  final int recommendedWeekday; // 1 (Mon) ~ 7 (Sun)
  final String recommendedTimeLabel; // e.g. "?曹? 20:00"
  final int peakSymptomDayOffset; // e.g. Day 2
  final String rationaleZh;
  final String rationaleEn;

  String rationale(AppLanguage lang) => lang.isZh ? rationaleZh : rationaleEn;
}

/// ?詨?憌??????雿單???撘?
class FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine {
  const FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine._();

  /// 閮??賣?敺洵 1 ~ 7 憭拍????雿撟喳??
  static List<DayCorrelationProfile> calculate7DayProfile({
    required List<DailySideEffectLog> sideEffectLogs,
    required List<InjectionRecord> injectionLogs,
  }) {
    // 靘??摨????    final List<InjectionRecord> sortedShots = List<InjectionRecord>.from(injectionLogs)
      ..sort((InjectionRecord a, InjectionRecord b) =>
          a.timestamp.compareTo(b.timestamp));

    // 撱箇? 7 憭拍?蝝臬?鞎臬?
    final List<List<DailySideEffectLog>> dayBuckets =
        List<List<DailySideEffectLog>>.generate(7, (_) => <DailySideEffectLog>[]);

    for (final DailySideEffectLog seLog in sideEffectLogs) {
      // ?曉閰脩??臭??函?????餈?銝蝑??      InjectionRecord? priorShot;
      for (final InjectionRecord shot in sortedShots.reversed) {
        if (shot.timestamp.isBefore(seLog.date) ||
            shot.timestamp.isAtSameMomentAs(seLog.date)) {
          priorShot = shot;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (priorShot != null) {
        final double elapsedDays =
            seLog.date.difference(priorShot.timestamp).inHours / 24.0;
        final int dayIndex = elapsedDays.floor(); // 0 ~ 6 撠? Day 1 ~ 7
        if (dayIndex >= 0 && dayIndex < 7) {
          dayBuckets[dayIndex].add(seLog);
        }
      }
    }

    // 閮?瘥蝯梯?撟喳? (?亦?祕璅?嚗?隞亥摨?Bateman ?乩誨?箇?鋆?)
    final List<DayCorrelationProfile> profiles = <DayCorrelationProfile>[];
    for (int d = 0; d < 7; d++) {
      final List<DailySideEffectLog> bucket = dayBuckets[d];
      if (bucket.isNotEmpty) {
        double sumNoise = 0;
        double sumNausea = 0;
        double sumFatigue = 0;
        double sumReflux = 0;
        double sumConstipation = 0;

        for (final DailySideEffectLog item in bucket) {
          sumNoise += item.foodNoiseSuppression;
          sumNausea += item.nauseaLevel;
          sumFatigue += item.fatigueLevel;
          sumReflux += item.acidRefluxLevel;
          sumConstipation += item.constipationLevel;
        }

        final int count = bucket.length;
        profiles.add(
          DayCorrelationProfile(
            postShotDay: d + 1,
            avgFoodNoiseSuppression:
                double.parse((sumNoise / count).toStringAsFixed(1)),
            avgNausea: double.parse((sumNausea / count).toStringAsFixed(1)),
            avgFatigue: double.parse((sumFatigue / count).toStringAsFixed(1)),
            avgAcidReflux: double.parse((sumReflux / count).toStringAsFixed(1)),
            avgConstipation:
                double.parse((sumConstipation / count).toStringAsFixed(1)),
            sampleCount: count,
          ),
        );
      } else {
        // ?典??身蝬??箸?蝺?(Day 1-2 ?陸憯?ay 6-7 ???)
        final List<double> defaultNoise = <double>[4.8, 4.6, 4.2, 3.8, 3.3, 2.6, 1.9];
        final List<double> defaultNausea = <double>[0.3, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1, 0.1, 0.0, 0.0];
        final List<double> defaultFatigue = <double>[0.4, 0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.1, 0.0];

        profiles.add(
          DayCorrelationProfile(
            postShotDay: d + 1,
            avgFoodNoiseSuppression: defaultNoise[d],
            avgNausea: defaultNausea[d],
            avgFatigue: defaultFatigue[d],
            avgAcidReflux: 0.1,
            avgConstipation: 0.2,
            sampleCount: 0,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    return profiles;
  }

  /// ?箸??嚗??犖?臭??券?撜啣???行??芣???畾?  static InjectionDayRecommendation recommendOptimalInjectionTiming({
    required List<DailySideEffectLog> sideEffectLogs,
    required List<InjectionRecord> injectionLogs,
    required AppLanguage lang,
  }) {
    final List<DayCorrelationProfile> profiles = calculate7DayProfile(
      sideEffectLogs: sideEffectLogs,
      injectionLogs: injectionLogs,
    );

    int worstDayOffset = 2; // ?身蝚?2 憭拚?撜?    double maxSeverity = -1;

    for (final DayCorrelationProfile p in profiles) {
      final double severity =
          math.max(p.avgFatigue, p.avgNausea) * 0.7 +
          (p.avgAcidReflux + p.avgConstipation) * 0.15;
      if (severity > maxSeverity) {
        maxSeverity = severity;
        worstDayOffset = p.postShotDay;
      }
    }

    int recommendedWeekday = DateTime.friday; // ?身?曹?
    if (worstDayOffset == 3) {
      recommendedWeekday = DateTime.thursday;
    } else if (worstDayOffset == 1) {
      recommendedWeekday = DateTime.friday;
    }

    final String dayNameZh =
        recommendedWeekday == DateTime.friday ? '?曹??? (20:00)' : '?勗??? (20:00)';
    final String dayNameEn =
        recommendedWeekday == DateTime.friday ? 'Friday Evening (8:00 PM)' : 'Thursday Evening (8:00 PM)';

    return InjectionDayRecommendation(
      recommendedWeekday: recommendedWeekday,
      recommendedTimeLabel: lang.isZh ? dayNameZh : dayNameEn,
      peakSymptomDayOffset: worstDayOffset,
      rationaleZh:
          '?豢???憿舐內?函????臭??刻??脣???賣?敺洵 $worstDayOffset 憭拇?憿航??遣霅啣??賣???摰???$dayNameZh嚗?銝擃陸?賢?望?∠????臭葉??瘨???,
      rationaleEn:
          'Personalized analytics detect your peak fatigue and mild nausea around Day $worstDayOffset post-shot. We recommend scheduling injections on $dayNameEn so peak symptoms coincide with weekend recovery.',
    );
  }
}

class _DailyFoodNoiseDialogState extends State<DailyFoodNoiseDialog> {

  String _getFoodNoiseDesc(int level, bool isZh) {

      case 3:
        return isZh ? '銝剖漲? (甇??甇?虜???)' : 'Moderate Control (Mild appetite)';
      case 2:
        return isZh ? '?敺格筑??(???單?梯正??' : 'Noise Creeping (Cravings returning)';
      default:
        return isZh ? '?撌典之/憌?券? (?交??＊???' : 'Breakthrough Hunger (Peak cravings)';
    }
  }

  Color _getFoodNoiseColor(int level) {

      default:

  }

  @override

            ),
            child: Icon(Icons.psychology_rounded, color: activeColor, size: 22),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              isZh ? '隞憭扯憌?閰?' : 'Daily Food Noise Check',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 520,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: <Widget>[
              Text(
                isZh
                    ? '憭扯銝剜??憌憌?敹菟憯蝔漲嚗?
                    : 'Level of mental chatter and craving suppression:',
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: Color(0xFF475569)),

                value: _fatigue,

              ),
              _buildSymptomSlider(
                label: isZh ? '???/?寞除' : 'Acid Reflux',
                value: _acidReflux,

              _buildSymptomSlider(

                value: _constipation,

          style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
            backgroundColor: activeColor,

      ],

  }

  Widget _buildSymptomSlider({
    required String label,
    required int value,
    required ValueChanged<int> onChanged,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6.0),
      child: Row(
        children: <Widget>[
          SizedBox(
            width: 95,
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Color(0xFF334155)),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: Slider(
              value: value.toDouble(),
              min: 0,
              max: 5,
              divisions: 5,
              activeColor: value > 2 ? const Color(0xFFEF4444) : const Color(0xFF0EA5E9),
              onChanged: (double v) {
                HapticFeedbackService.selectionTick();
                onChanged(v.round());
              },
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(
            width: 24,
            child: Text(
              '$value',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                color: value > 2 ? const Color(0xFFEF4444) : const Color(0xFF334155),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/core/haptic_feedback_service.dart ===

import 'dart:io' show Platform;
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

/// 閫豢???憿???
enum HapticFeedbackType {
  selection,
  light,
  medium,
  heavy,
  milestone,
  success,
  warning,
  error,
}

/// ?怎?蝝凝閫豢?????嚗芋?祉移撖??券?頛芣?璇唳???
class HapticFeedbackService {
  const HapticFeedbackService._();

  /// ?典???嚗?雿輻?閮剖???梢???????嚗?  static bool isHapticsEnabled = true;

  /// ?之?脤?萄? 06嚗葫閰行??? Web ?啣??拍?蝯楠靽風??  static bool get _isShielded {
    if (kIsWeb) return true;
    try {
      if (Platform.environment.containsKey('FLUTTER_TEST')) return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /// 頛凝朣憚皛渡????冽皛▼瘥??潦雿凝隤選?
  static Future<void> selectionTick() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// 頛漲璈１?嚗?澆虜?刻??寞?蝐文???
  static Future<void> lightImpact() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// 銝剖漲?嚗?潭芋撘????暹??交雯 <-> 銋曄?瘜∟ˊ嚗?  static Future<void> mediumImpact() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// 瘛勗漲璈１??嚗?潭??⊥????之瑼?蝣箄?嚗?  static Future<void> heavyImpact() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// 50 Units 暺???蝣?荔???銵??孛??
  static Future<void> milestoneHit() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 65));
      await HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// ?????閫貉死??嚗??質釭/憌脫偌/??噶摰?嚗?  static Future<void> successPattern() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 75));
      await HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// ??頞?/?梢?詨潮????望乩?霅血嚗??100 Units嚗?  static Future<void> warningPattern() async {
    if (!isHapticsEnabled || _isShielded) return;
    try {
      await HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
      await HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
      await HapticFeedback.vibrate();
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}

/// ??撠???餃側璈１閫豢??批?剁?璅⊥蝎曉?瘣餃??賢??餃側嚗?class SyringeHapticEngine {
  SyringeHapticEngine();

  double _lastTrackedUnits = 0.0;
  DateTime _lastHapticTime = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);

  /// 蝭瘚?嚗甇Ｘ??Ｘ???敹恍? Taptic Engine 雿??餃?嚗?  static const int _throttleIntervalMs = 28;

  /// ?身?折餈質馱?餃漲
  void reset(double currentUnits) {
    _lastTrackedUnits = currentUnits;
    _lastHapticTime = DateTime.now();
  }

  /// ?園?蝑??貊?????澆嚗??撠??孛閬粹撠?  void onUnitsChanged(double newUnits) {
    if (!HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled) return;

    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    if (now.difference(_lastHapticTime).inMilliseconds < _throttleIntervalMs) {
      return;
    }

    final int prevFloor = _lastTrackedUnits.floor();
    final int newFloor = newUnits.floor();

    // 1. 100 Units 頞??拍?璆菟?霅血嚗?遛蝞∴?
    if (_lastTrackedUnits <= 100.0 && newUnits > 100.0) {
      _lastHapticTime = now;
      _lastTrackedUnits = newUnits;
      HapticFeedbackService.warningPattern();
      return;
    }

    // 2. 50 Units 暺???蝣??豢?嚗楊頞?50 ?潮??菜?蝺?
    if ((_lastTrackedUnits < 50.0 && newUnits >= 50.0) ||
        (_lastTrackedUnits > 50.0 && newUnits <= 50.0)) {
      _lastHapticTime = now;
      _lastTrackedUnits = newUnits;
      HapticFeedbackService.milestoneHit();
      return;
    }

    // 3. 10 Units ?湔銝餃摨阡???    if (newFloor ~/ 10 != prevFloor ~/ 10) {
      _lastHapticTime = now;
      _lastTrackedUnits = newUnits;
      HapticFeedbackService.lightImpact();
      return;
    }

    // 4. 5 Units 甈∪摨血凝朣憚皛渡?
    if (newFloor ~/ 5 != prevFloor ~/ 5) {
      _lastHapticTime = now;
      _lastTrackedUnits = newUnits;
      HapticFeedbackService.selectionTick();
      return;
    }

    _lastTrackedUnits = newUnits;
  }

  /// ?豢?璅???璅惜??璈１閫豢? (2.5mg, 5.0mg...)
  void onDoseSelected(double doseMg) {
    if (!HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled) return;
    HapticFeedbackService.lightImpact();
  }

  /// ???銝蝑?????楛摨衣Ⅱ隤?
  void onShotLogged() {
    if (!HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled) return;
    HapticFeedbackService.heavyImpact();
  }

  /// 摰??鞈?憌脫偌/??噶????停??銵?  void onHabitCompleted() {
    if (!HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled) return;
    HapticFeedbackService.successPattern();
  }
}

/// ?典?閫貉死??閮剖??批?剁?蝜潭 ChangeNotifier嚗??UI ???單????嚗?class HapticSettingsController extends ChangeNotifier {
  HapticSettingsController._();

  static final HapticSettingsController instance =
      HapticSettingsController._();

  bool get isEnabled => HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled;

  set isEnabled(bool value) {
    if (HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled == value) return;
    HapticFeedbackService.isHapticsEnabled = value;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void toggle() {
    isEnabled = !isEnabled;
  }

  /// 閫豢?撅內?芣炎?寞?嚗?閮剖??霈蝙?刻岫?岫??
  Future<void> previewHaptic(HapticFeedbackType type) async {
    switch (type) {
      case HapticFeedbackType.selection:
        await HapticFeedbackService.selectionTick();
      case HapticFeedbackType.light:
        await HapticFeedbackService.lightImpact();
      case HapticFeedbackType.medium:
        await HapticFeedbackService.mediumImpact();
      case HapticFeedbackType.heavy:
        await HapticFeedbackService.heavyImpact();
      case HapticFeedbackType.milestone:
        await HapticFeedbackService.milestoneHit();
      case HapticFeedbackType.success:
        await HapticFeedbackService.successPattern();
      case HapticFeedbackType.warning:
      case HapticFeedbackType.error:
        await HapticFeedbackService.warningPattern();
    }
  }
}


=== lib/core/healthkit_service.dart ===

import 'dart:io' show Platform;
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

/// ?舀??Apple HealthKit ?豢?憿?
enum HealthDataType {
  bodyMass('HKQuantityTypeIdentifierBodyMass', 'kg'),
  dietaryProtein('HKQuantityTypeIdentifierDietaryProtein', 'g'),
  dietaryWater('HKQuantityTypeIdentifierDietaryWater', 'mL');

  const HealthDataType(this.identifier, this.defaultUnit);
  final String identifier;
  final String defaultUnit;
}

/// HealthKit 甈??????enum HealthAuthStatus {
  notDetermined,
  authorized,
  denied,
  restricted,
}

/// ?亙熒?豢?璅?銝霈?擃?@immutable
class HealthDataSample {
  const HealthDataSample({
    required this.type,
    required this.value,
    required this.unit,
    required this.timestamp,
    this.source = 'Apple Health',
  });

  final HealthDataType type;
  final double value;
  final String unit;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final String source;

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return <String, dynamic>{
      'identifier': type.identifier,
      'value': value,
      'unit': unit,
      'timestamp': timestamp.toIso8601String(),
      'source': source,
    };
  }
}

/// Apple HealthKit ??璈????甇亙???蝚血? Apple HealthKit Review 閬?嚗?class HealthKitService {
  HealthKitService._();

  static final HealthKitService instance = HealthKitService._();

  static const MethodChannel _channel =
      MethodChannel('com.doseflow.app/healthkit');

  HealthAuthStatus _authStatus = HealthAuthStatus.notDetermined;
  HealthAuthStatus get authStatus => _authStatus;

  /// ?之?脤?萄? 06嚗葫閰行??? Web ?啣??拍?蝯楠靽風??  static bool get _isShielded {
    if (kIsWeb) return true;
    try {
      if (Platform.environment.containsKey('FLUTTER_TEST')) return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /// ?閮擃摨瑟?澈嚗?皜祈岫瘝??? iOS ?撟喳雿輻嚗?  final Map<HealthDataType, List<HealthDataSample>> _virtualStore =
      <HealthDataType, List<HealthDataSample>>{
    HealthDataType.bodyMass: <HealthDataSample>[],
    HealthDataType.dietaryProtein: <HealthDataSample>[],
    HealthDataType.dietaryWater: <HealthDataSample>[],
  };

  /// ??Apple HealthKit 隢?擃????質釭?ㄡ瘞港?霈撖急???  Future<HealthAuthStatus> requestAuthorization() async {
    if (_isShielded) {
      _authStatus = HealthAuthStatus.authorized;
      return _authStatus;
    }

    try {
      final String? result =
          await _channel.invokeMethod<String>('requestAuthorization', <String, dynamic>{
        'readTypes': HealthDataType.values.map((HealthDataType t) => t.identifier).toList(),
        'writeTypes': HealthDataType.values.map((HealthDataType t) => t.identifier).toList(),
      });
      if (result == 'authorized') {
        _authStatus = HealthAuthStatus.authorized;
      } else if (result == 'denied') {
        _authStatus = HealthAuthStatus.denied;
      } else {
        _authStatus = HealthAuthStatus.restricted;
      }
    } catch (_) {
      _authStatus = HealthAuthStatus.notDetermined;
    }
    return _authStatus;
  }

  /// 霈??Apple Health 銝剜??啁?銝蝑?????(kg)
  Future<double?> fetchLatestWeightKg() async {
    if (_isShielded) {
      final List<HealthDataSample> samples =
          _virtualStore[HealthDataType.bodyMass]!;
      if (samples.isNotEmpty) {
        return samples.last.value;
      }
      return null;
    }

    try {
      final double? weight = await _channel.invokeMethod<double>(
        'fetchLatestSample',
        <String, dynamic>{
          'identifier': HealthDataType.bodyMass.identifier,
        },
      );
      return weight;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// 霈???亥 HealthKit 蝝航??????質釭 (g)
  Future<int> fetchTodayProteinGrams() async {
    if (_isShielded) {
      final List<HealthDataSample> samples =
          _virtualStore[HealthDataType.dietaryProtein]!;
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      int total = 0;
      for (final HealthDataSample s in samples) {
        if (s.timestamp.year == now.year &&
            s.timestamp.month == now.month &&
            s.timestamp.day == now.day) {
          total += s.value.round();
        }
      }
      return total;
    }

    try {
      final int? protein = await _channel.invokeMethod<int>(
        'fetchTodayCumulative',
        <String, dynamic>{
          'identifier': HealthDataType.dietaryProtein.identifier,
        },
      );
      return protein ?? 0;
    } catch (_) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  /// 霈???亥 HealthKit 蝝航??ㄡ瘞湧? (mL)
  Future<int> fetchTodayWaterMl() async {
    if (_isShielded) {
      final List<HealthDataSample> samples =
          _virtualStore[HealthDataType.dietaryWater]!;
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      int total = 0;
      for (final HealthDataSample s in samples) {
        if (s.timestamp.year == now.year &&
            s.timestamp.month == now.month &&
            s.timestamp.day == now.day) {
          total += s.value.round();
        }
      }
      return total;
    }

    try {
      final int? water = await _channel.invokeMethod<int>(
        'fetchTodayCumulative',
        <String, dynamic>{
          'identifier': HealthDataType.dietaryWater.identifier,
        },
      );
      return water ?? 0;
    } catch (_) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  /// ???神擃?蝝? Apple Health
  Future<bool> writeWeightSampleKg(double weightKg, DateTime timestamp) async {
    final HealthDataSample sample = HealthDataSample(
      type: HealthDataType.bodyMass,
      value: weightKg,
      unit: 'kg',
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );

    if (_isShielded) {
      _virtualStore[HealthDataType.bodyMass]!.add(sample);
      return true;
    }

    try {
      final bool? success = await _channel.invokeMethod<bool>(
        'writeSample',
        sample.toMap(),
      );
      return success ?? false;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// ???神憌脫偌蝝? Apple Health
  Future<bool> writeWaterSampleMl(int waterMl, DateTime timestamp) async {
    final HealthDataSample sample = HealthDataSample(
      type: HealthDataType.dietaryWater,
      value: waterMl.toDouble(),
      unit: 'mL',
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );

    if (_isShielded) {
      _virtualStore[HealthDataType.dietaryWater]!.add(sample);
      return true;
    }

    try {
      final bool? success = await _channel.invokeMethod<bool>(
        'writeSample',
        sample.toMap(),
      );
      return success ?? false;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// ???神?鞈芰?? Apple Health
  Future<bool> writeProteinSampleGrams(int proteinGrams, DateTime timestamp) async {
    final HealthDataSample sample = HealthDataSample(
      type: HealthDataType.dietaryProtein,
      value: proteinGrams.toDouble(),
      unit: 'g',
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );

    if (_isShielded) {
      _virtualStore[HealthDataType.dietaryProtein]!.add(sample);
      return true;
    }

    try {
      final bool? success = await _channel.invokeMethod<bool>(
        'writeSample',
        sample.toMap(),
      );
      return success ?? false;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }
}

/// HealthKit ?踵?撘??甇乩葉璅?蝜潭 ChangeNotifier嚗??UI 擃?/敺桃???蝮恍??嚗?class HealthSyncController extends ChangeNotifier {
  HealthSyncController._();

  static final HealthSyncController instance = HealthSyncController._();

  bool _isSyncing = false;
  DateTime? _lastSyncTime;
  double? _latestSyncedWeightKg;
  int _todaySyncedProteinGrams = 0;
  int _todaySyncedWaterMl = 0;

  bool get isSyncing => _isSyncing;
  DateTime? get lastSyncTime => _lastSyncTime;
  double? get latestSyncedWeightKg => _latestSyncedWeightKg;
  int get todaySyncedProteinGrams => _todaySyncedProteinGrams;
  int get todaySyncedWaterMl => _todaySyncedWaterMl;
  HealthAuthStatus get authStatus => HealthKitService.instance.authStatus;

  /// ?瑁??券????郊嚗????圈???亥??質釭?ㄡ瘞渡敞閮?
  Future<void> syncAll() async {
    if (_isSyncing) return;
    _isSyncing = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      if (authStatus != HealthAuthStatus.authorized) {
        await HealthKitService.instance.requestAuthorization();
      }

      final double? weight =
          await HealthKitService.instance.fetchLatestWeightKg();
      if (weight != null) {
        _latestSyncedWeightKg = weight;
      }

      _todaySyncedProteinGrams =
          await HealthKitService.instance.fetchTodayProteinGrams();
      _todaySyncedWaterMl =
          await HealthKitService.instance.fetchTodayWaterMl();
      _lastSyncTime = DateTime.now();
    } finally {
      _isSyncing = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  /// 閮?銝阡???甇仿?? HealthKit
  Future<bool> recordWeight(double weightKg, {DateTime? timestamp}) async {
    final DateTime time = timestamp ?? DateTime.now();
    final bool success =
        await HealthKitService.instance.writeWeightSampleKg(weightKg, time);
    if (success) {
      _latestSyncedWeightKg = weightKg;
      _lastSyncTime = DateTime.now();
      notifyListeners();
    }
    return success;
  }

  /// 閮?銝阡???甇仿ㄡ瘞湧???HealthKit
  Future<bool> recordWater(int waterMl, {DateTime? timestamp}) async {
    final DateTime time = timestamp ?? DateTime.now();
    final bool success =
        await HealthKitService.instance.writeWaterSampleMl(waterMl, time);
    if (success) {
      _todaySyncedWaterMl += waterMl;
      _lastSyncTime = DateTime.now();
      notifyListeners();
    }
    return success;
  }

  /// 閮?銝阡???甇亥??質釭??? HealthKit
  Future<bool> recordProtein(int proteinGrams, {DateTime? timestamp}) async {
    final DateTime time = timestamp ?? DateTime.now();
    final bool success = await HealthKitService.instance
        .writeProteinSampleGrams(proteinGrams, time);
    if (success) {
      _todaySyncedProteinGrams += proteinGrams;
      _lastSyncTime = DateTime.now();
      notifyListeners();
    }
    return success;
  }
}

  const ComplianceDocType(this.titleEn, this.titleZh);
  final String titleEn;
  final String titleZh;

  String title(AppLanguage lang) => lang.isZh ? titleZh : titleEn;
}

/// 瘜??辣鞈?頛?

    required this.type,
    required this.title,

  /// ?Ｙ?蝢??雿輻????甈曇??怎??痊蝬脤? terms_of_service.html
  static String generateTermsOfServiceHtml(AppLanguage lang) {
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;
    final String title = isZh
        ? '?衣????Pro - 雿輻????甈曇??怎??痊'

/// App ?找???瘜???撖拙?閬炎閬?閰望?
class LegalComplianceViewerDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const LegalComplianceViewerDialog({
    super.key,
    this.initialDocType = ComplianceDocType.privacyPolicy,
  });

  final ComplianceDocType initialDocType;

  /// 銝?萄? HTML 瑼?撖阡?撖怠?祆?撠??桅?嚗??單??典惇??GitHub Pages嚗?  static Future<String?> exportHtmlFilesToDisk(AppLanguage lang) async {
    if (kIsWeb) return null;
    try {
      final String privacyHtml =
          LegalComplianceHub.generatePrivacyPolicyHtml(lang);
      final String termsHtml =
          LegalComplianceHub.generateTermsOfServiceHtml(lang);

      final File privacyFile = File('privacy_policy.html');
      final File termsFile = File('terms_of_service.html');

      await privacyFile.writeAsString(privacyHtml, flush: true);
      await termsFile.writeAsString(termsHtml, flush: true);

      return privacyFile.path;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  @override
  State<LegalComplianceViewerDialog> createState() =>
      _LegalComplianceViewerDialogState();
}

class _LegalComplianceViewerDialogState
    extends State<LegalComplianceViewerDialog> {
  late ComplianceDocType _selectedType;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _selectedType = widget.initialDocType;
  }

  String _getDocumentBody(AppLanguage lang) {
    switch (_selectedType) {
      case ComplianceDocType.privacyPolicy:
        return LegalComplianceHub.generatePrivacyPolicyHtml(lang);
      case ComplianceDocType.termsOfService:
        return LegalComplianceHub.generateTermsOfServiceHtml(lang);
      case ComplianceDocType.fdaCitations:
        return '${LegalComplianceHub.fdaTirzepatideDetails}\n'
            '${LegalComplianceHub.fdaSemaglutideDetails}\n'
            '${LegalComplianceHub.fdaLiraglutideDetails}';
      case ComplianceDocType.appStoreReviewNotes:
        return LegalComplianceHub.generateAppStoreReviewNotes(lang);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppLanguage lang = context.locale.language;
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;
    final String contentText = _getDocumentBody(lang);

    return AlertDialog(
      title: Row(
        children: <Widget>[
          const Icon(Icons.gavel_rounded, color: Color(0xFF0EA5E9), size: 24),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),

            child: Text(
              isZh ? '瘜?????撖拐葉璅? : 'Legal Compliance & Review Hub',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 620,
        height: 480,
        child: Column(
          children: <Widget>[
            // ??辣憿???璅惜
            SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              child: Row(
                children: ComplianceDocType.values.map((ComplianceDocType type) {
                  final bool isSelected = type == _selectedType;
                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6.0),
                    child: ChoiceChip(
                      label: Text(
                        type.title(lang),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,

                      ),
                      selected: isSelected,
                      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _selectedType = type),
                    ),
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            // ???批捆?汗?
            Expanded(
              child: Container(

                  color: const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),

                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFCBD5E1)),
                ),
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  child: SelectableText(
                    contentText,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 11.0,
                      fontFamily: 'Consolas',
                      height: 1.45,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: <Widget>[
        // ?臬撖阡? HTML ??

          onPressed: () async {

                await LegalComplianceViewerDialog.exportHtmlFilesToDisk(lang);

                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(
                    path != null
                        ? (isZh
                            ? '?? 撌脣?祆??桅??? privacy_policy.html嚗?湔閮恣??
                            : '?? Exported privacy_policy.html to project root!')
                        : (isZh
                            ? '?? 撖怠憭望?????Web ?啣?'
                            : '?? Write restricted or in Web environment'),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }
          },
          icon: const Icon(Icons.file_download_outlined, size: 16),
          label: Text(isZh ? '?臬 HTML 瑼?' : 'Export HTML Files'),
        ),
        // 銴ˊ蝝?摮???        TextButton.icon(
          onPressed: () {
            Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: contentText));
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(

                ),

=== lib/core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart ===

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:ui' as ui;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'app_localization.dart';

/// GLP-1 ?亦??摮詨??貉? FDA 隞踹?摰儔 (?萄儐 Apple App Review Guideline 1.4.1)
enum Glp1Medication {
  tirzepatide(
    id: 'tirzepatide',
    displayNameZh: '?璅?Mounjaro / Zepbound (Tirzepatide)',
    displayNameEn: 'Tirzepatide (Mounjaro / Zepbound)',
    halfLifeHours: 120.0, // 5 days (120 hrs)
    absorptionRateKa: 0.055, // Tmax ~ 36 hrs
    peakHoursTmax: 36.0,
    fdaCitationTitle:
        'Eli Lilly Mounjaro (Tirzepatide) FDA Prescribing Information - Section 12.3 Pharmacokinetics',
    fdaCitationUrl: 'https://pi.lilly.com/us/mounjaro-uspi.pdf',
  ),
  semaglutide(
    id: 'semaglutide',
    displayNameZh: '?梁???Wegovy / ????Ozempic (Semaglutide)',
    displayNameEn: 'Semaglutide (Wegovy / Ozempic)',
    halfLifeHours: 168.0, // 7 days (168 hrs)
    absorptionRateKa: 0.038, // Tmax ~ 48 hrs
    peakHoursTmax: 48.0,
    fdaCitationTitle:
        'Novo Nordisk Ozempic/Wegovy (Semaglutide) FDA Prescribing Information - Section 12.3 Pharmacokinetics',
    fdaCitationUrl: 'https://www.novo-pi.com/ozempic.pdf',
  ),
  liraglutide(
    id: 'liraglutide',
    displayNameZh: '?????仿? Saxenda (Liraglutide)',
    displayNameEn: 'Liraglutide (Saxenda Daily)',
    halfLifeHours: 13.0, // 13 hrs
    absorptionRateKa: 0.22, // Tmax ~ 11 hrs
    peakHoursTmax: 11.0,
    fdaCitationTitle:
        'Novo Nordisk Saxenda (Liraglutide) FDA Prescribing Information - Section 12.3 Pharmacokinetics',
    fdaCitationUrl: 'https://www.novo-pi.com/saxenda.pdf',
  );

  const Glp1Medication({
    required this.id,
    required this.displayNameZh,
    required this.displayNameEn,
    required this.halfLifeHours,
    required this.absorptionRateKa,
    required this.peakHoursTmax,
    required this.fdaCitationTitle,
    required this.fdaCitationUrl,
  });

  final String id;
  final String displayNameZh;
  final String displayNameEn;
  final double halfLifeHours;
  final double absorptionRateKa;
  final double peakHoursTmax;
  final String fdaCitationTitle;
  final String fdaCitationUrl;

  /// 瘨??撣豢 k_e = ln(2) / t_{1/2}
  double get eliminationRateKe => math.log(2.0) / halfLifeHours;

  /// 靘??嗅?隤頂??亦?迂
  String displayName(AppLanguage lang) =>
      lang.isZh ? displayNameZh : displayNameEn;
}

/// ?賣??其???嚗?港葉?梢?隤??脩′憛?憚?踵?蝞?嚗?enum InjectionSite {
  leftUpperAbdomen('撌虫???(?輸??? 5cm)', 'Left Upper Abdomen'),
  leftLowerAbdomen('撌虫???(?輸??? 5cm)', 'Left Lower Abdomen'),
  rightUpperAbdomen('?喃???(?輸??? 5cm)', 'Right Upper Abdomen'),
  rightLowerAbdomen('?喃???(?輸??? 5cm)', 'Right Lower Abdomen'),
  leftThigh('撌血之?踹?憭', 'Left Anterior Thigh'),
  rightThigh('?喳之?踹?憭', 'Right Anterior Thigh'),
  leftArm('撌虫?????, 'Left Upper Arm'),
  rightArm('?喃?????, 'Right Upper Arm');

  const InjectionSite(this.labelZh, this.labelEn);
  final String labelZh;
  final String labelEn;

  String label(AppLanguage lang) => lang.isZh ? labelZh : labelEn;
}

/// ?桃??賣?蝝???芋??@immutable
class InjectionRecord {
  const InjectionRecord({
    required this.id,
    required this.timestamp,
    required this.medication,
    required this.doseMg,
    required this.site,
    this.painLevel = 0,
    this.batchOrVialNote,
  });

  final String id;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final Glp1Medication medication;
  final double doseMg;
  final InjectionSite site;
  final int painLevel;
  final String? batchOrVialNote;
}

/// ??摨?瞈漲?見暺?靘?銵函鼓鋆賡???14 憭拙祕蝺??芯? 7 憭拚?皜祈?蝺?
@immutable
class ConcentrationPoint {
  const ConcentrationPoint({
    required this.timestamp,
    required this.estimatedMg,
    required this.isFuturePrediction,
    this.triggeredDoseMg,
  });

  final DateTime timestamp;
  final double estimatedMg;
  final bool isFuturePrediction;
  final double? triggeredDoseMg;
}

/// ?詨??亦??摮豢?蝞???(Bateman Function + Superposition Principle)
class PharmacokineticsEngine {
  const PharmacokineticsEngine._();

  /// ??滲??隞??閬?嚗?0 憭?= 1440 撠?嚗?  static const int lookbackWindowHours = 60 * 24;

  /// 靘??????App 蝯曹?撠惇?脩巨
  static Color colorForDose(double doseMg) {
    if (doseMg <= 2.5) return const Color(0xFF9B87F5); // 2.5mg ?啗﹝?換
    if (doseMg <= 5.0) return const Color(0xFF0EA5E9); // 5.0mg 瘚瑟???    if (doseMg <= 7.5) return const Color(0xFF10B981); // 7.5mg 蝧∠?蝬?    if (doseMg <= 10.0) return const Color(0xFFF97316); // 10.0mg ??璈?    if (doseMg <= 12.5) return const Color(0xFFEC4899); // 12.5mg ?怎蝝?    return const Color(0xFF8B5CF6); // 15.0mg+ ?振蝝?  }

  /// 閮??格活?賣??函???elapsedHours 撠?敺?擃畾???C_i(t)
  /// ?砍?: C_i(t) = D_i * (k_a / (k_a - k_e)) * (exp(-k_e * t) - exp(-k_a * t))
  static double singleDoseConcentrationAt({
    required double doseMg,
    required Glp1Medication medication,
    required double elapsedHours,
  }) {
    if (elapsedHours <= 0.0 ||
        elapsedHours > lookbackWindowHours ||
        doseMg <= 0.0) {
      return 0.0;
    }

    final double ka = medication.absorptionRateKa;
    final double ke = medication.eliminationRateKe;

    if ((ka - ke).abs() < 1e-6) {
      final double fallback =
          doseMg * ka * elapsedHours * math.exp(-ke * elapsedHours);
      return fallback.isFinite && fallback > 0.0 ? fallback : 0.0;
    }

    final double scaling = ka / (ka - ke);
    final double decayDiff =
        math.exp(-ke * elapsedHours) - math.exp(-ka * elapsedHours);
    final double concentration = doseMg * scaling * decayDiff;

    if (!concentration.isFinite || concentration < 0.0) {
      return 0.0;
    }
    return concentration;
  }

  /// 閮?隞颱???暺?queryTime ?????蜇瞈漲 C_total(T) = \sum C_i(T - t_i)
  static double totalConcentrationAt({
    required DateTime queryTime,
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
  }) {
    double totalMg = 0.0;
    for (final InjectionRecord log in logs) {
      final double diffHours =
          queryTime.difference(log.timestamp).inMinutes / 60.0;
      if (diffHours > 0.0 && diffHours <= lookbackWindowHours) {
        totalMg += singleDoseConcentrationAt(
          doseMg: log.doseMg,
          medication: log.medication,
          elapsedHours: diffHours,
        );
      }
    }
    return double.parse(totalMg.toStringAsFixed(4));
  }

  /// ?Ｙ?? pastDays 憭抵?芯? futureDays 憭拍?撟單?瞈漲?脩?摨扳?摨?
  static List<ConcentrationPoint> generateTimelineSeries({
    required List<InjectionRecord> logs,
    required DateTime now,
    int pastDays = 14,
    int futureDays = 7,
    int stepHours = 3,
  }) {
    final int safeStep = math.max(1, stepHours);
    final DateTime startTime = now.subtract(Duration(days: pastDays));
    final DateTime endTime = now.add(Duration(days: futureDays));
    final int totalHours = endTime.difference(startTime).inHours;

    final List<ConcentrationPoint> series = <ConcentrationPoint>[];
    for (int h = 0; h <= totalHours; h += safeStep) {
      final DateTime pointTime = startTime.add(Duration(hours: h));
      final bool isFuture = pointTime.isAfter(now);
      final double conc = totalConcentrationAt(
        queryTime: pointTime,
        logs: logs,
      );

      double? triggeredDose;
      for (final InjectionRecord log in logs) {
        final double diffHrs =
            (pointTime.difference(log.timestamp).inMinutes / 60.0).abs();
        if (diffHrs < (safeStep / 2.0)) {
          triggeredDose = log.doseMg;
          break;
        }
      }

      series.add(
        ConcentrationPoint(
          timestamp: pointTime,
          estimatedMg: conc,
          isFuturePrediction: isFuture,
          triggeredDoseMg: triggeredDose,
        ),
      );
    }
    return series;
  }

  /// ?脩′憛雿?憚?踹遣霅堆??曉????銝???遣霅圈雿?  static InjectionSite recommendNextSite(List<InjectionRecord> logs) {
    if (logs.isEmpty) {
      return InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen;
    }
    final List<InjectionRecord> sorted = List<InjectionRecord>.from(logs)
      ..sort((InjectionRecord a, InjectionRecord b) =>
          b.timestamp.compareTo(a.timestamp));
    final InjectionSite lastSite = sorted.first.site;
    const List<InjectionSite> rotationOrder = InjectionSite.values;
    final int currentIndex = rotationOrder.indexOf(lastSite);
    final int nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % rotationOrder.length;
    return rotationOrder[nextIndex];
  }
}

/// 擃?亦瞈漲?單??脩?蝜芸???(璅⊥ Swift Charts AreaMark + LineMark + RuleMark)
class PharmacokineticsChartPainter extends CustomPainter {
  PharmacokineticsChartPainter({
    required this.series,
    required this.primaryColor,
    required this.nowLabel,
  });

  final List<ConcentrationPoint> series;
  final Color primaryColor;
  final String nowLabel;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    if (series.length < 2) return;

    const double topPad = 24.0;
    const double bottomPad = 22.0;
    final double chartHeight = math.max(10.0, size.height - topPad - bottomPad);
    final double chartWidth = size.width;

    double maxMg = 0.5;
    for (final ConcentrationPoint pt in series) {
      if (pt.estimatedMg > maxMg) {
        maxMg = pt.estimatedMg;
      }
    }
    maxMg *= 1.18; // ????澆蝛粹?

    // 1. 蝜芾ˊ瘞游像?蝺?    final Paint gridPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF94A3B8).withValues(alpha: 0.18)
      ..strokeWidth = 1.0;

    for (int i = 0; i <= 3; i++) {
      final double y = topPad + chartHeight * (i / 3.0);
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(chartWidth, y), gridPaint);
    }

    Offset pointToOffset(int index) {
      final double x = (index / (series.length - 1)) * chartWidth;
      final double normalizedY = (series[index].estimatedMg / maxMg).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
      final double y = topPad + chartHeight * (1.0 - normalizedY);
      return Offset(x, y);
    }

    int nowIndex = series.indexWhere((ConcentrationPoint p) => p.isFuturePrediction) - 1;
    if (nowIndex < 0) nowIndex = series.length - 1;

    // 2. 蝜芾ˊ? 14 憭拙祕蝺?瞍詨惜?Ｙ? (AreaMark)
    final Path pastLinePath = Path();
    final Path pastAreaPath = Path();
    final double baselineY = topPad + chartHeight;

    for (int i = 0; i <= nowIndex; i++) {
      final Offset pt = pointToOffset(i);
      if (i == 0) {
        pastLinePath.moveTo(pt.dx, pt.dy);
        pastAreaPath.moveTo(pt.dx, baselineY);
        pastAreaPath.lineTo(pt.dx, pt.dy);
      } else {
        final Offset prev = pointToOffset(i - 1);
        final double midX = (prev.dx + pt.dx) / 2.0;
        pastLinePath.cubicTo(midX, prev.dy, midX, pt.dy, pt.dx, pt.dy);
        pastAreaPath.cubicTo(midX, prev.dy, midX, pt.dy, pt.dx, pt.dy);
      }
    }

    final Offset nowOffset = pointToOffset(nowIndex);
    pastAreaPath.lineTo(nowOffset.dx, baselineY);
    pastAreaPath.close();

    final Paint areaPaint = Paint()
      ..shader = ui.Gradient.linear(
        const Offset(0, topPad),
        Offset(0, baselineY),
        <Color>[
          primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.38),
          primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.02),
        ],
      )
      ..style = PaintingStyle.fill;
    canvas.drawPath(pastAreaPath, areaPaint);

    final Paint solidLinePaint = Paint()
      ..color = primaryColor
      ..strokeWidth = 2.8
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;
    canvas.drawPath(pastLinePath, solidLinePaint);

    // 3. 蝜芾ˊ?芯? 7 憭拙?銵唳??葫?? (Dashed LineMark)
    if (nowIndex < series.length - 1) {
      final Paint dashedPaint = Paint()
        ..color = primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.65)
        ..strokeWidth = 2.2
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;

      for (int i = nowIndex; i < series.length - 1; i++) {
        if (i.isEven) {
          final Offset p1 = pointToOffset(i);
          final Offset p2 = pointToOffset(i + 1);
          canvas.drawLine(p1, p2, dashedPaint);
        }
      }
    }

    // 4. 蝜芾ˊ??ow嚗?剁???蝷箇? (RuleMark) ?銝?摨血?暺?    final Paint rulePaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF0F172A).withValues(alpha: 0.45)
      ..strokeWidth = 1.4;
    canvas.drawLine(
      Offset(nowOffset.dx, topPad - 6),
      Offset(nowOffset.dx, baselineY),
      rulePaint,
    );

    canvas.drawCircle(
      nowOffset,
      6.5,
      Paint()..color = primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
    );
    canvas.drawCircle(
      nowOffset,
      4.0,
      Paint()..color = primaryColor,
    );

    final TextPainter tp = TextPainter(
      text: TextSpan(
        text: nowLabel,
        style: const TextStyle(
          fontSize: 10.5,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
          color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
        ),
      ),
      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
    )..layout();

    final double labelX = (nowOffset.dx - tp.width / 2).clamp(4.0, chartWidth - tp.width - 4.0);
    tp.paint(canvas, Offset(labelX, 4.0));
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant PharmacokineticsChartPainter oldDelegate) {
    return oldDelegate.series != series ||
        oldDelegate.primaryColor != primaryColor ||
        oldDelegate.nowLabel != nowLabel;
  }
}
