/// ?? iOS 17 SwiftUI + Swift Charts 摰?祕雿?箏
class SwiftReferenceExporter {
  const SwiftReferenceExporter._();

  static const String swiftChartsSourceCode = r'''
import SwiftUI
import Charts
import SwiftData

enum GLP1MedicationType: String, Codable, CaseIterable {
    case tirzepatide = "tirzepatide"
    case semaglutide = "semaglutide"
    case liraglutide = "liraglutide"

    var halfLifeHours: Double {
        switch self {
        case .tirzepatide: return 120.0 // 5 days
        case .semaglutide: return 168.0 // 7 days
        case .liraglutide: return 13.0  // 13 hours
        }
    }

    var absorptionRateKa: Double {
        switch self {
        case .tirzepatide: return 0.055 // Tmax ~36h
        case .semaglutide: return 0.038 // Tmax ~48h
        case .liraglutide: return 0.220 // Tmax ~11h
        }
    }

    var eliminationRateKe: Double {
        log(2.0) / halfLifeHours
    }
}

@Model
final class InjectionLog {
    var id: UUID
    var timestamp: Date
    var medicationRaw: String
    var doseMg: Double
    var siteRaw: String

    init(timestamp: Date, medication: GLP1MedicationType, doseMg: Double, siteRaw: String) {
        self.id = UUID()
        self.timestamp = timestamp
        self.medicationRaw = medication.rawValue
        self.doseMg = doseMg
        self.siteRaw = siteRaw
    }

    var medication: GLP1MedicationType {
        GLP1MedicationType(rawValue: medicationRaw) ?? .tirzepatide
    }
}

struct PKDataPoint: Identifiable {
    let id = UUID()
    let date: Date
    let concentrationMg: Double
    let isForecast: Bool
}

struct PharmacokineticsEngine {
    static let lookbackHours: Double = 60.0 * 24.0

    /// Bateman Function: C(t) = D * (ka / (ka - ke)) * (exp(-ke * t) - exp(-ka * t))
    static func singleDoseConcentration(doseMg: Double, med: GLP1MedicationType, elapsedHours t: Double) -> Double {
        guard t > 0, t <= lookbackHours, doseMg > 0 else { return 0.0 }
        let ka = med.absorptionRateKa
        let ke = med.eliminationRateKe
        guard abs(ka - ke) > 1e-6 else {
            return max(0.0, doseMg * ka * t * exp(-ke * t))
        }
        let scale = ka / (ka - ke)
        let level = doseMg * scale * (exp(-ke * t) - exp(-ka * t))
        return max(0.0, level)
    }

    /// Superposition Principle across 60-day window
    static func totalConcentration(at queryDate: Date, logs: [InjectionLog]) -> Double {
        logs.reduce(0.0) { acc, log in
            let elapsedHours = queryDate.timeIntervalSince(log.timestamp) / 3600.0
            return acc + singleDoseConcentration(doseMg: log.doseMg, med: log.medication, elapsedHours: elapsedHours)
        }
    }

    static func generateSeries(logs: [InjectionLog], now: Date = .now) -> [PKDataPoint] {
        let cal = Calendar.current
        guard let start = cal.date(byAdding: .day, value: -14, to: now),
              let end = cal.date(byAdding: .day, value: 7, to: now) else { return [] }
        var points: [PKDataPoint] = []
        var cursor = start
        while cursor <= end {
            let level = totalConcentration(at: cursor, logs: logs)
            points.append(PKDataPoint(date: cursor, concentrationMg: level, isForecast: cursor > now))
            cursor = cursor.addingTimeInterval(3 * 3600)
        }
        return points
    }
}

struct ConcentrationChartView: View {
    let logs: [InjectionLog]
    let isProUnlocked: Bool
    @State private var now: Date = .now

    var body: some View {
        let series = PharmacokineticsEngine.generateSeries(logs: logs, now: now)
        let currentMg = PharmacokineticsEngine.totalConcentration(at: now, logs: logs)

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            Text(String(format: "Active GLP-1 Level: %.2f mg", currentMg))
                .font(.headline)

            Chart {
                ForEach(series) { pt in
                    if !pt.isForecast {
                        AreaMark(
                            x: .value("Date", pt.date),
                            y: .value("mg", pt.concentrationMg)
                        )
                        .interpolationMethod(.catmullRom)
                        .foregroundStyle(.linearGradient(
                            colors: [.blue.opacity(0.38), .blue.opacity(0.02)],
                            startPoint: .top,
                            endPoint: .bottom
                        ))
                    }
                    LineMark(
                        x: .value("Date", pt.date),
                        y: .value("mg", pt.concentrationMg)
                    )
                    .interpolationMethod(.catmullRom)
                    .lineStyle(StrokeStyle(lineWidth: 2.5, dash: pt.isForecast ? [5, 4] : []))
                    .foregroundStyle(.blue)
                }
                RuleMark(x: .value("Now", now))
                    .foregroundStyle(.primary.opacity(0.5))
                    .annotation(position: .top) {
                        Text("NOW").font(.caption2.bold())
                    }
            }
            .frame(height: 190)
            .blur(radius: isProUnlocked ? 0 : 7)
        }
        .padding()
    }
}
''';

  static void showSwiftCodeModal(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext dialogContext) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: Row(
            children: <Widget>[
              const Icon(Icons.apple_rounded, size: 22),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  context.locale.tr(
                    'iOS 17 SwiftUI + Swift Charts ??蝔?蝣?,
                    'iOS 17 SwiftUI + Swift Charts Native Code',
                  ),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          content: const SizedBox(
            width: 560,
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: SelectableText(
                swiftChartsSourceCode,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontFamily: 'Consolas',
                  fontSize: 11.5,
                  height: 1.38,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          actions: <Widget>[
            TextButton.icon(
              onPressed: () {
                Clipboard.setData(
                  const ClipboardData(text: swiftChartsSourceCode),
                );
                Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop();
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text(
                      context.locale.tr(
                        '撌脰?鋆賢???Swift Charts 蝔?蝣潸?芾票蝪選?',
                        'Copied full Swift Charts code to clipboard!',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 16),
              label: Text(context.locale.tr('銝?菔?鋆?Swift 隞?Ⅳ', 'Copy Swift Code')),
            ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(),
              child: Text(context.locale.tr('??', 'Close')),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

/// 閮??鞎餅獢?撅?enum SubscriptionTier {
  free('free'),
  monthly('com.doseflow.app.monthly'),
  annual('com.doseflow.app.annual'),
  lifetime('com.doseflow.app.lifetime');

  const SubscriptionTier(this.productId);
  final String productId;

  bool get isPaid => this != SubscriptionTier.free;
}

/// 鞈潸眺鈭斗???望????enum PurchaseStatus {
  idle,
  purchasing,
  restored,
  expired,
  failed,
}

/// ?桅???閬???@immutable
class ProductItem {
  const ProductItem({
    required this.tier,
    required this.productId,
    required this.titleZh,
    required this.titleEn,
    required this.priceUsdString,
    required this.priceTwdString,
    required this.hasFreeTrial,
    required this.trialDays,
    required this.isBestValue,
  });

  final SubscriptionTier tier;
  final String productId;
  final String titleZh;
  final String titleEn;
  final String priceUsdString;
  final String priceTwdString;
  final bool hasFreeTrial;
  final int trialDays;
  final bool isBestValue;
}

/// 摰???桅?摰儔 (?萄儐 PRD 蝚?5 蝭銋??寧??
class DoseFlowProductCatalog {
  const DoseFlowProductCatalog._();

  static const ProductItem monthly = ProductItem(
    tier: SubscriptionTier.monthly,
    productId: 'com.doseflow.app.monthly',
    titleZh: '?漲閮',
    titleEn: 'Monthly Subscription',
    priceUsdString: r'$6.99',
    priceTwdString: r'NT$ 150',
    hasFreeTrial: false,
    trialDays: 0,
    isBestValue: false,
  );

  static const ProductItem annual = ProductItem(
    tier: SubscriptionTier.annual,
    productId: 'com.doseflow.app.annual',
    titleZh: '撟游漲閮 (銝餅?刻)',
    titleEn: 'Annual Subscription (Best Value)',
    priceUsdString: r'$39.99',
    priceTwdString: r'NT$ 990',
    hasFreeTrial: true,
    trialDays: 3,
    isBestValue: true,
  );

  static const ProductItem lifetime = ProductItem(
    tier: SubscriptionTier.lifetime,
    productId: 'com.doseflow.app.lifetime',
    titleZh: '蝯澈鞎瑟?寞?',
    titleEn: 'Lifetime Access',
    priceUsdString: r'$79.99',
    priceTwdString: r'NT$ 1,990',
    hasFreeTrial: false,
    trialDays: 0,
    isBestValue: false,
  );

  static const List<ProductItem> all = <ProductItem>[
    monthly,
    annual,
    lifetime,
  ];

  static ProductItem getByTier(SubscriptionTier tier) {
    return all.firstWhere(
      (ProductItem p) => p.tier == tier,
      orElse: () => annual,
    );
  }
}

/// 撠平???∟??潭???霅芋??@immutable
class ProEntitlementInfo {
  const ProEntitlementInfo({
    required this.isProUnlocked,
    required this.tier,
    this.originalPurchaseDate,
    this.expirationDate,
    this.isInTrial = false,
    this.receiptHash,
  });

  final bool isProUnlocked;
  final SubscriptionTier tier;
  final DateTime? originalPurchaseDate;
  final DateTime? expirationDate;
  final bool isInTrial;
  final String? receiptHash;

  /// ?祥?冽???身甈?
  factory ProEntitlementInfo.free() {
    return const ProEntitlementInfo(
      isProUnlocked: false,
      tier: SubscriptionTier.free,
    );
  }

  /// ?拚?閰衣???予??  int get daysRemaining {
    if (tier == SubscriptionTier.lifetime) return 99999;
    if (expirationDate == null) return 0;
    final Duration diff = expirationDate!.difference(DateTime.now());
    return diff.isNegative ? 0 : diff.inDays;
  }

  /// 撽?甈??桀??臬??????  bool get isActive {
    if (!isProUnlocked) return false;
    if (tier == SubscriptionTier.lifetime) return true;
    if (expirationDate == null) return false;
    return expirationDate!.isAfter(DateTime.now());
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return <String, dynamic>{
      'isProUnlocked': isProUnlocked,
      'tier': tier.name,
      'originalPurchaseDate': originalPurchaseDate?.toIso8601String(),
      'expirationDate': expirationDate?.toIso8601String(),
      'isInTrial': isInTrial,
      'receiptHash': receiptHash,
    };
  }

  factory ProEntitlementInfo.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return ProEntitlementInfo(
      isProUnlocked: map['isProUnlocked'] as bool? ?? false,
      tier: SubscriptionTier.values.firstWhere(
        (SubscriptionTier t) => t.name == map['tier'],
        orElse: () => SubscriptionTier.free,
      ),
      originalPurchaseDate: map['originalPurchaseDate'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(map['originalPurchaseDate'] as String)
          : null,
      expirationDate: map['expirationDate'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(map['expirationDate'] as String)
          : null,
      isInTrial: map['isInTrial'] as bool? ?? false,
      receiptHash: map['receiptHash'] as String?,
    );
  }
}

/// ?詨?鞈潸眺???望???嗅嚗hangeNotifier ?踵?撘瑽??舀 StoreKit 2 / RevenueCat ?砍瘝?璅⊥嚗?class PurchaseService extends ChangeNotifier {
  PurchaseService._() {
    _initialize();
  }

  static final PurchaseService instance = PurchaseService._();

  ProEntitlementInfo _currentEntitlement = ProEntitlementInfo.free();
  PurchaseStatus _status = PurchaseStatus.idle;
  String? _lastErrorMessage;

  ProEntitlementInfo get currentEntitlement => _currentEntitlement;
  PurchaseStatus get status => _status;
  String? get lastErrorMessage => _lastErrorMessage;
  bool get isProUnlocked => _currentEntitlement.isActive;

  /// ???璈???霅?蝝??園? 0 瘥怎???嚗撟唾?訾?嚗?  void _initialize() {
    _currentEntitlement = ProEntitlementInfo.free();
  }

  /// ?瑁?鞈潸眺瘚?嚗????3 憭拙?鞎餉岫?刻??寞??唳?閮?嚗?  Future<bool> purchase(SubscriptionTier tier) async {
    if (tier == SubscriptionTier.free) return false;

    _status = PurchaseStatus.purchasing;
    _lastErrorMessage = null;
    notifyListeners();

    // 璅⊥鈭斗?撱園 (瘝??啣? ~600ms)
    await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 600));

    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    DateTime? expireDate;
    bool isInTrial = false;

    switch (tier) {
      case SubscriptionTier.monthly:
        expireDate = now.add(const Duration(days: 30));
        isInTrial = false;
      case SubscriptionTier.annual:
        // 撟游漲閮鈭急? 3 憭拙?鞎餉岫??(3 憭抵岫??+ 365 憭?
        expireDate = now.add(const Duration(days: 368));
        isInTrial = true;
      case SubscriptionTier.lifetime:
        expireDate = null;
        isInTrial = false;
      case SubscriptionTier.free:
        return false;
    }

    // ???砍?脣?嗆???嚗ase64 閬?嚗?    final String rawReceipt = jsonEncode(<String, dynamic>{
      'productId': tier.productId,
      'purchaseDate': now.toIso8601String(),
      'expirationDate': expireDate?.toIso8601String(),
      'isInTrial': isInTrial,
    });
    final String encodedReceipt = base64Encode(utf8.encode(rawReceipt));

    _currentEntitlement = ProEntitlementInfo(
      isProUnlocked: true,
      tier: tier,
      originalPurchaseDate: now,
      expirationDate: expireDate,
      isInTrial: isInTrial,
      receiptHash: encodedReceipt,
    );

    _status = PurchaseStatus.idle;
    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }

  /// ?Ｗ儔撌脰頃鞎琿???(Restore Purchases)
  Future<bool> restorePurchases() async {
    _status = PurchaseStatus.purchasing;
    notifyListeners();

    await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));

    // ?亦?歇??鞎餌??????Ｗ儔嚗?雁??鞎?    if (_currentEntitlement.isProUnlocked && _currentEntitlement.isActive) {
      _status = PurchaseStatus.restored;
      notifyListeners();
      return true;
    } else {
      _status = PurchaseStatus.idle;
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// 皜祈岫撠嚗?????Pro ???  void toggleProForTesting() {
    if (_currentEntitlement.isProUnlocked) {
      _currentEntitlement = ProEntitlementInfo.free();
    } else {
      _currentEntitlement = ProEntitlementInfo(
        isProUnlocked: true,
        tier: SubscriptionTier.annual,
        originalPurchaseDate: DateTime.now(),
        expirationDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 368)),
        isInTrial: true,
        receiptHash: base64Encode(utf8.encode('sandbox_test_receipt')),
      );
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

/// ?嗆???摰?扯??脣撽???(?萄儐?之?脤?萄? 09嚗ase64 ?湔摮?甇???萄?)
class ReceiptValidator {
  const ReceiptValidator._();

  /// ?湔撽? Base64 ?澆?摰?改???鈭Ⅳ?賡?  static bool isValidBase64(String? input) {
    if (input == null || input.isEmpty) return false;
    final String clean = input.replaceAll('\r', '').replaceAll('\n', '').trim();
    if (clean.length % 4 != 0) return false;
    return RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$').hasMatch(clean);
  }

  /// ?祆??嗆?閫?????扳炎撽?  static bool verifyLocalReceipt(String? receiptHash) {
    if (!isValidBase64(receiptHash)) return false;
    try {
      final String decoded = utf8.decode(base64Decode(receiptHash!));
      final Map<String, dynamic> data =
          jsonDecode(decoded) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final String? productId = data['productId'] as String?;
      if (productId == null || !productId.startsWith('com.doseflow.app')) {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }
}

/// ?平霈甈?摰?嚗?銝剔恣? App ?祥??vs Pro 撠平???賡?瑼鳴?
class ProAccessGate {
  const ProAccessGate._();

  /// ?祥?????刻岫?冽活?訾???  static const int freeSyringeCalcQuota = 3;

  /// ?臬??瑼Ｚ?擃?單??亦瞈漲???豢蝺?(Bateman Curve)
  static bool canViewPkCurve({required bool isProUnlocked}) {
    return isProUnlocked;
  }

  /// ?臬???⊿??嗡蝙?券?蝑?蝞 (?祥?冽?憭?3 甈?
  static bool canUseSyringeCalculator({
    required bool isProUnlocked,
    required int usageCount,
  }) {
    if (isProUnlocked) return true;
    return usageCount < freeSyringeCalcQuota;
  }

  /// ?臬??閫?????擃?????(Dose-Colored Weight Chart)
  static bool canViewDoseColoredWeight({required bool isProUnlocked}) {
    return isProUnlocked;
  }

  /// ?臬??雿輻 iOS 17 獢??摰?Ｗ?撌亙 (Widgets)
  static bool canAccessWidgets({required bool isProUnlocked}) {
    return isProUnlocked;
  }

  /// ?臬???臬?那銝???怎? PDF ?梯”
  static bool canExportDoctorSummary({required bool isProUnlocked}) {
    return isProUnlocked;
  }
}


=== lib/features/calculator/syringe_calculator_view.dart ===

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/app_localization.dart';
import '../../core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';

/// ?????券?璅∪???嚗?/// 1. premixed: ?暹?銴??亦撌脫?蝷箸?摨?(靘? 5 mg/mL ??10 mg/mL)
/// 2. reconstitution: 銋曄??亦 (mg) + ?芾????瘞?(Bacteriostatic Water, mL)
enum SyringeCalcMode {
  premixed,
  reconstitution,
}

/// Toyota Poka-Yoke ?脣?撽????enum SyringeValidationStatus {
  valid,
  invalidInput,
  exceedsSyringeCapacity,
  microDosePrecisionNotice,
}

/// ???桐???銝霈??芋??@immutable
class SyringeCalculationResult {
  const SyringeCalculationResult({
    required this.status,
    required this.effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl,
    required this.volumeMl,
    required this.u100Units,
    required this.targetDoseMg,
    required this.dosesPerVial,
  });

  final SyringeValidationStatus status;
  final double effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl;
  final double volumeMl;
  final double u100Units;
  final double targetDoseMg;
  final double dosesPerVial;

  bool get isValid =>
      status == SyringeValidationStatus.valid ||
      status == SyringeValidationStatus.microDosePrecisionNotice;
}

/// 撣貊?亦???翰??閮剔???@immutable
class VialPreset {
  const VialPreset({
    required this.labelZh,
    required this.labelEn,
    required this.mode,
    required this.vialMassMg,
    required this.waterMl,
    required this.concentrationMgPerMl,
    required this.targetDoseMg,
  });

  final String labelZh;
  final String labelEn;
  final SyringeCalcMode mode;
  final double vialMassMg;
  final double waterMl;
  final double concentrationMgPerMl;
  final double targetDoseMg;

  String label(AppLanguage lang) => lang.isZh ? labelZh : labelEn;
}

/// ?詨?????????蝞???(U-100 璅??啣雀蝝?蝑? 1.0 mL = 100 Units)
class SyringeMathEngine {
  const SyringeMathEngine._();

  static const double maxStandardSyringeMl = 1.0;
  static const double maxStandardUnits = 100.0;

  static const List<VialPreset> commonPresets = <VialPreset>[
    VialPreset(
      labelZh: '5 mg/mL ??2.5 mg (50 ??',
      labelEn: '5 mg/mL -> 2.5 mg (50 Units)',
      mode: SyringeCalcMode.premixed,
      vialMassMg: 10.0,
      waterMl: 2.0,
      concentrationMgPerMl: 5.0,
      targetDoseMg: 2.5,
    ),
    VialPreset(
      labelZh: '10 mg/mL ??5.0 mg (50 ??',
      labelEn: '10 mg/mL -> 5.0 mg (50 Units)',
      mode: SyringeCalcMode.premixed,
      vialMassMg: 20.0,
      waterMl: 2.0,
      concentrationMgPerMl: 10.0,
      targetDoseMg: 5.0,
    ),
    VialPreset(
      labelZh: '銋曄? 10mg + 2mL 瘞?-> ??2.5mg',
      labelEn: '10mg Vial + 2mL BAC -> 2.5mg',
      mode: SyringeCalcMode.reconstitution,
      vialMassMg: 10.0,
      waterMl: 2.0,
      concentrationMgPerMl: 5.0,
      targetDoseMg: 2.5,
    ),
    VialPreset(
      labelZh: '銋曄? 15mg + 3mL 瘞?-> ??7.5mg',
      labelEn: '15mg Vial + 3mL BAC -> 7.5mg',
      mode: SyringeCalcMode.reconstitution,
      vialMassMg: 15.0,
      waterMl: 3.0,
      concentrationMgPerMl: 5.0,
      targetDoseMg: 7.5,
    ),
  ];

  /// ?瑁? U-100 ???潭閮???Poka-Yoke ?拍???瑼Ｘ
  static SyringeCalculationResult calculate({
    required SyringeCalcMode mode,
    required double concentrationMgPerMl,
    required double vialMassMg,
    required double bacteriostaticWaterMl,
    required double targetDoseMg,
  }) {
    if (targetDoseMg <= 0.0 || !targetDoseMg.isFinite) {
      return const SyringeCalculationResult(
        status: SyringeValidationStatus.invalidInput,
        effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl: 0.0,
        volumeMl: 0.0,
        u100Units: 0.0,
        targetDoseMg: 0.0,
        dosesPerVial: 0.0,
      );
    }

    double effectiveConcentration = 0.0;
    double totalVialMass = vialMassMg;

    if (mode == SyringeCalcMode.premixed) {
      if (concentrationMgPerMl <= 0.0 || !concentrationMgPerMl.isFinite) {
        return const SyringeCalculationResult(
          status: SyringeValidationStatus.invalidInput,
          effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl: 0.0,
          volumeMl: 0.0,
          u100Units: 0.0,
          targetDoseMg: 0.0,
          dosesPerVial: 0.0,
        );
      }
      effectiveConcentration = concentrationMgPerMl;
      totalVialMass = concentrationMgPerMl * 2.0;
    } else {
      if (vialMassMg <= 0.0 ||
          bacteriostaticWaterMl <= 0.0 ||
          !vialMassMg.isFinite ||
          !bacteriostaticWaterMl.isFinite) {
        return const SyringeCalculationResult(
          status: SyringeValidationStatus.invalidInput,
          effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl: 0.0,
          volumeMl: 0.0,
          u100Units: 0.0,
          targetDoseMg: 0.0,
          dosesPerVial: 0.0,
        );
      }
      effectiveConcentration = vialMassMg / bacteriostaticWaterMl;
      totalVialMass = vialMassMg;
    }

    final double volumeMl = targetDoseMg / effectiveConcentration;
    final double u100Units = volumeMl * 100.0;
    final double dosesPerVial = totalVialMass / targetDoseMg;

    if (!volumeMl.isFinite || !u100Units.isFinite || volumeMl <= 0.0) {
      return const SyringeCalculationResult(
        status: SyringeValidationStatus.invalidInput,
        effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl: 0.0,
        volumeMl: 0.0,
        u100Units: 0.0,
        targetDoseMg: 0.0,
        dosesPerVial: 0.0,
      );
    }

    if (u100Units > maxStandardUnits + 1e-6) {
      return SyringeCalculationResult(
        status: SyringeValidationStatus.exceedsSyringeCapacity,
        effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl: effectiveConcentration,
        volumeMl: volumeMl,
        u100Units: u100Units,
        targetDoseMg: targetDoseMg,
        dosesPerVial: dosesPerVial,
      );
    }

    final SyringeValidationStatus status = u100Units < 2.0
        ? SyringeValidationStatus.microDosePrecisionNotice
        : SyringeValidationStatus.valid;

    return SyringeCalculationResult(
      status: status,
      effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl:
          double.parse(effectiveConcentration.toStringAsFixed(3)),
      volumeMl: double.parse(volumeMl.toStringAsFixed(3)),
      u100Units: double.parse(u100Units.toStringAsFixed(1)),
      targetDoseMg: targetDoseMg,
      dosesPerVial: double.parse(dosesPerVial.toStringAsFixed(1)),
    );
  }
}

/// 100 Units (1.0 mL) 璅? U-100 ?啣雀蝝?蝑??摨衣鼓?
class U100SyringePainter extends CustomPainter {
  const U100SyringePainter({
    required this.units,
    required this.liquidColor,
    required this.isOverCapacity,
  });

  final double units;
  final Color liquidColor;
  final bool isOverCapacity;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final double clampedRatio = (units / 100.0).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    final Color activeColor =
        isOverCapacity ? const Color(0xFFEF4444) : liquidColor;

    const double needleAreaWidth = 34.0;
    const double plungerGripWidth = 28.0;
    const double barrelLeft = needleAreaWidth; // ?⊥???const 靽桅ˇ蝚?    final double barrelRight = size.width - plungerGripWidth - 16.0;
    final double barrelWidth = math.max(80.0, barrelRight - barrelLeft);
    final double centerY = size.height / 2.0;
    const double barrelHeight = 46.0;
    final double barrelTop = centerY - barrelHeight / 2.0;
    final double barrelBottom = centerY + barrelHeight / 2.0;

    // 1. 蝜芾ˊ撌血???摨?(Needle & Hub)
    final Paint needlePaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF64748B)
      ..strokeWidth = 2.0
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;
    // ?⊥??箏???Offset(4.0, centerY)嚗?銝? const
    canvas.drawLine(
      Offset(4.0, centerY),
      Offset(barrelLeft - 8.0, centerY),
      needlePaint,
    );

    final RRect hubRRect = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(
      Rect.fromLTWH(barrelLeft - 10.0, centerY - 8.0, 10.0, 16.0),
      const Radius.circular(3.0),
    );
    canvas.drawRRect(
      hubRRect,
      Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFF97316).withValues(alpha: 0.85),
    );

    // 2. 蝜芾ˊ????憭恣?
    final RRect barrelRRect = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(
      Rect.fromLTWH(barrelLeft, barrelTop, barrelWidth, barrelHeight),
      const Radius.circular(6.0),
    );
    canvas.drawRRect(
      barrelRRect,
      Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
    );

    // 3. 蝜芾ˊ?賢??瘨脫 (Liquid Fill)
    final double fillWidth = barrelWidth * clampedRatio;
    if (fillWidth > 0.5) {
      final Rect liquidRect = Rect.fromLTWH(
        barrelLeft,
        barrelTop + 2.0,
        fillWidth,
        barrelHeight - 4.0,
      );
      canvas.drawRect(
        liquidRect,
        Paint()..color = activeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
      );
    }

    // 4. 蝜芾ˊ暺璈∟?瘣餃??剛??冽▼ (Plunger Stopper & Rod)
    final double plungerX = barrelLeft + fillWidth;
    final Rect stopperRect = Rect.fromLTWH(
      plungerX,
      barrelTop + 2.0,
      7.0,
      barrelHeight - 4.0,
    );
    canvas.drawRect(
      stopperRect,
      Paint()..color = const Color(0xFF1E293B),
    );

    final Paint rodPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF94A3B8)
      ..strokeWidth = 8.0;
    canvas.drawLine(
      Offset(plungerX + 7.0, centerY),
      Offset(size.width - 8.0, centerY),
      rodPaint,
    );

    // ??撠曄垢?冽????蕉??    canvas.drawLine(
      Offset(barrelRight, barrelTop - 8.0),
      Offset(barrelRight, barrelBottom + 8.0),
      Paint()
        ..color = const Color(0xFF64748B)
        ..strokeWidth = 3.5
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round,
    );
    canvas.drawLine(
      Offset(size.width - 8.0, barrelTop + 4.0),
      Offset(size.width - 8.0, barrelBottom - 4.0),
      Paint()
        ..color = const Color(0xFF475569)
        ..strokeWidth = 4.5
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round,
    );

    // 5. 蝜芾ˊ 0 ~ 100 Units 蝎曉??餃漲蝺??詨?
    final Paint tickPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF334155)
      ..strokeWidth = 1.2;

    for (int u = 0; u <= 100; u += 5) {
      final double x = barrelLeft + (u / 100.0) * barrelWidth;
      final bool isMajor = (u % 10 == 0);
      final double tickLen = isMajor ? 12.0 : 6.5;
      canvas.drawLine(
        Offset(x, barrelTop),
        Offset(x, barrelTop + tickLen),
        tickPaint,
      );

      if (u % 20 == 0 || u == 50) {
        final TextStyle numStyle = u == 50
            ? const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 9.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
              )
            : const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 9.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
              );

        final TextPainter tp = TextPainter(
          text: TextSpan(
            text: '$u',
            style: numStyle,
          ),
          textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
        )..layout();
        tp.paint(canvas, Offset(x - tp.width / 2.0, barrelBottom - 14.0));
      }
    }

    // 6. 蝜芾ˊ??憭???鈭桃璅摨行?蝷箇?
    canvas.drawRRect(
      barrelRRect,
      Paint()
        ..color = const Color(0xFF475569)
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 1.6,
    );

    if (units > 0) {
      canvas.drawLine(
        Offset(plungerX, barrelTop - 6.0),
        Offset(plungerX, barrelBottom + 6.0),
        Paint()
          ..color = activeColor
          ..strokeWidth = 2.4,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant U100SyringePainter oldDelegate) {
    return oldDelegate.units != units ||
        oldDelegate.liquidColor != liquidColor ||
        oldDelegate.isOverCapacity != isOverCapacity;
  }
}

/// 鈭粹? 8 憭扳釣撠雿憚?蹂???嚗??蝷箔?甈⊥??暺?撱箄降?祆活蝬?嚗?class BodySiteRotationSelector extends StatelessWidget {

                        : 'Smart Injection Site Rotation Map',

                  : 'Avoid 2 inches (5cm) around the navel. Red = Last Shot, Green = Recommended Next:',

            ),

                      borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: isSelected
                            ? const Color(0xFF0EA5E9)
                            : isRec
                                ? const Color(0xFF10B981).withValues(alpha: 0.6)
                                : const Color(0xFFE2E8F0),
                        width: isSelected ? 1.8 : 1.1,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: <Widget>[
                        Container(
                          width: 10,
                          height: 10,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: dotColor,
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            site.label(lang),
                            maxLines: 2,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 11.5,
                              fontWeight: isSelected || isRec
                                  ? FontWeight.w700
                                  : FontWeight.w500,
                              color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              }).toList(),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegendDot({required Color color, required String label}) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: <Widget>[
        Container(
          width: 9,
          height: 9,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 5),
        Text(
          label,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 11.5,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            color: Color(0xFF334155),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Tab 2 摰?嚗?隤犖擃雿憚?輯? U-100 ???桐?????class ShotLogAndCalculatorView extends StatefulWidget {
  const ShotLogAndCalculatorView({
    super.key,
    required this.logs,
    required this.isProUnlocked,
    required this.onAddLog,
    required this.onRequestUnlockPro,
  });

  final List<InjectionRecord> logs;
  final bool isProUnlocked;
  final ValueChanged<InjectionRecord> onAddLog;
  final VoidCallback onRequestUnlockPro;

  @override
  State<ShotLogAndCalculatorView> createState() =>
      _ShotLogAndCalculatorViewState();
}

class _ShotLogAndCalculatorViewState extends State<ShotLogAndCalculatorView> {
  static const List<double> _standardDoses = <double>[
    2.5,
    5.0,
    7.5,
    10.0,
    12.5,
    15.0,
  ];

  SyringeCalcMode _calcMode = SyringeCalcMode.premixed;
  double _concentrationMgPerMl = 5.0;
  double _vialMassMg = 10.0;
  double _bacteriostaticWaterMl = 2.0;
  double _targetDoseMg = 2.5;

  late Glp1Medication _selectedMedication;
  late InjectionSite _selectedSite;
  final int _painLevel = 1;
  int _freeCalcRemaining = 3;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // ?萄儐 Apple Guideline 1.4.1嚗?閮剖?撣嗅銝?甈⊥?????亦嚗?銝蜓??血???
    if (widget.logs.isNotEmpty) {
      _selectedMedication = widget.logs.first.medication;
      _targetDoseMg = widget.logs.first.doseMg;
    } else {
      _selectedMedication = Glp1Medication.tirzepatide;
      _targetDoseMg = 2.5;
    }
    _selectedSite = PharmacokineticsEngine.recommendNextSite(widget.logs);
  }

  void _consumeFreeQuotaIfNeeded() {
    if (!widget.isProUnlocked && _freeCalcRemaining > 0) {
      setState(() {
        _freeCalcRemaining--;
      });
    }
  }

  void _applyPreset(VialPreset preset) {
    if (!widget.isProUnlocked && _freeCalcRemaining <= 0) {
      widget.onRequestUnlockPro();
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _calcMode = preset.mode;
      _vialMassMg = preset.vialMassMg;
      _bacteriostaticWaterMl = preset.waterMl;
      _concentrationMgPerMl = preset.concentrationMgPerMl;
      _targetDoseMg = preset.targetDoseMg;
      if (!widget.isProUnlocked && _freeCalcRemaining > 0) {
        _freeCalcRemaining--;
      }
    });
  }

  void _handleRecordShot() {
    final InjectionRecord newRecord = InjectionRecord(
      id: 'shot_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      medication: _selectedMedication,
      doseMg: _targetDoseMg,
      site: _selectedSite,
      painLevel: _painLevel,
    );
    widget.onAddLog(newRecord);
    setState(() {
      _selectedSite = PharmacokineticsEngine.recommendNextSite(
        <InjectionRecord>[newRecord, ...widget.logs],
      );
    });
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(
          context.locale.tr(
            '??撌脫憓?${_targetDoseMg.toStringAsFixed(1)} mg ?賣?蝝??擃隞???脩?撌脣??堆?',
            '??Logged ${_targetDoseMg.toStringAsFixed(1)} mg shot! Live PK curve updated.',
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppStrings s = context.strings;
    final AppLanguage lang = context.locale.language;
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;

    final SyringeCalculationResult calcResult = SyringeMathEngine.calculate(
      mode: _calcMode,
      concentrationMgPerMl: _concentrationMgPerMl,
      vialMassMg: _vialMassMg,
      bacteriostaticWaterMl: _bacteriostaticWaterMl,
      targetDoseMg: _targetDoseMg,
    );

    final Color doseColor = PharmacokineticsEngine.colorForDose(_targetDoseMg);
    final InjectionSite? lastSite =
        widget.logs.isNotEmpty ? widget.logs.first.site : null;
    final InjectionSite recommendedSite =
        PharmacokineticsEngine.recommendNextSite(widget.logs);
    final bool isCalcLocked = !widget.isProUnlocked && _freeCalcRemaining <= 0;

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      children: <Widget>[
        // 1. U-100 ???桐??????蝞?∠?
        Card(
          elevation: 0,
          color: Colors.white,

            side: BorderSide(

            ),
          ),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                Row(

                              : 'Effective: ${calcResult.effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl} mg/mL | ~${calcResult.dosesPerVial} doses per vial',

              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: <Widget>[
                Text(
                  isZh ? '?啣?隞?賣?蝝?? : 'Log Today\'s Injection',

                    fontSize: 15.0,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                    color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                DropdownButtonFormField<Glp1Medication>(
                  initialValue: _selectedMedication,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: isZh ? '?賣??亦??' : 'Active GLP-1 Medication',
                    border: const OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                    ),
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: 12,
                      vertical: 10,
                    ),
                  ),
                  items: Glp1Medication.values.map((Glp1Medication med) {
                    return DropdownMenuItem<Glp1Medication>(
                      value: med,
                      child: Text(
                        med.displayName(lang),
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                  onChanged: (Glp1Medication? val) {
                    if (val != null) {
                      setState(() => _selectedMedication = val);
                    }
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  child: FilledButton.icon(
                    onPressed: _handleRecordShot,
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.add_task_rounded),
                    label: Text(
                      isZh
                          ? '蝣箄??賣? ${_targetDoseMg.toStringAsFixed(1)} mg ??${_selectedSite.label(lang)}'
                          : 'Log ${_targetDoseMg.toStringAsFixed(1)} mg at ${_selectedSite.label(lang)}',
                    ),
                    style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: doseColor,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSliderRow({
    required String label,
    required String valueText,
    required double value,
    required double min,
    required double max,
    required int divisions,
    required Color activeColor,
    required ValueChanged<double> onChanged,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: <Widget>[
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: <Widget>[
            Text(
              label,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: Color(0xFF334155),
              ),
            ),
            Text(
              valueText,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13.0,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                color: activeColor,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        Slider(
          value: value.clamp(min, max),
          min: min,
          max: max,
          divisions: divisions,
          activeColor: activeColor,
          onChanged: onChanged,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/app_localization.dart';
import '../../core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';

/// iPhone Duo / ??閮剖?憪踵???
enum DuoDisplayPosture {
  /// 5.4 ???Ｗ??????(?格?璆菟?雿?
  foldedOuter,

  /// 7.6 ???之?Ｗ????(??銝行?憭極)
  unfoldedDualPane,

  /// 獢?詨??? 90 摨行芋撘?(?????質撘?)
  tabletopHalfFold,
}

/// ?箸????Ｗ?憪踵??祝摨虫??Ｘ葫??class DuoDeviceDetector {
  const DuoDeviceDetector._();

  /// ?文??曉潘?撅??扯撟祝摨阡虜憭扳 600dp
  static const double unfoldedWidthThreshold = 600.0;

  /// ?寞??嗅? MediaQuery 撠箏站???函?閮剖?憪踵?
  static DuoDisplayPosture getPosture(BuildContext context) {
    final Size size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;
    final Orientation orientation = MediaQuery.of(context).orientation;

    // 撖砍漲憭扳?曉潸??箏???7.6 ?之?Ｗ?
    if (size.width >= unfoldedWidthThreshold && (orientation == Orientation.portrait || orientation == Orientation.landscape)) {
      // ?仿?摨西?撖砍漲?亥?銝??潭帖????????舫???
      return DuoDisplayPosture.unfoldedDualPane;
    }

    return DuoDisplayPosture.foldedOuter;
  }

  /// ?臬?瑕?撅?憭扯撟征??  static bool isLargeScreen(BuildContext context) {
    return getPosture(context) == DuoDisplayPosture.unfoldedDualPane;
  }
}

/// iPhone Duo ???芷??擃捆??(Adaptive Dual-Pane Scaffold)
class DuoAdaptiveScaffold extends StatelessWidget {
  const DuoAdaptiveScaffold({
    super.key,
    required this.primaryPane,
    required this.secondaryPane,
    this.tabletopGuidePane,
    this.appBar,
    this.bottomNavigationBar,
  });

  final Widget primaryPane;
  final Widget secondaryPane;
  final Widget? tabletopGuidePane;
  final PreferredSizeWidget? appBar;
  final Widget? bottomNavigationBar;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final DuoDisplayPosture posture = DuoDeviceDetector.getPosture(context);

    // 1. 7.6 ???之?Ｗ?嚗??曉椰?喲?甈???蝔曹?撅
    if (posture == DuoDisplayPosture.unfoldedDualPane) {
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: appBar,
        body: SafeArea(
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: <Widget>[
              // 撌行?嚗敹蝺???
              Expanded(
                flex: 5,
                child: primaryPane,
              ),
              // 銝剝?敺桀???
              Container(
                width: 1.2,
                color: const Color(0xFFE2E8F0),
              ),
              // ?單?嚗??隅?Ｕ?暺??扯???
              Expanded(
                flex: 5,
                child: secondaryPane,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        bottomNavigationBar: bottomNavigationBar,
      );
    }

    // 2. 5.4 ?????Ｗ???皞?璈?璅??格?瘚?雿?
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: appBar,
      body: primaryPane,
      bottomNavigationBar: bottomNavigationBar,
    );
  }
}

/// iPhone Duo 5.4 ???Ｗ????Ｘ?翰?????class DuoOuterQuickActionCard extends StatelessWidget {

  });

  final InjectionSite recommendedSite;
  final double targetDoseMg;
  final double calculatedUnits;
  final double calculatedVolumeMl;
  final VoidCallback onConfirmInjection;

  void _openTabletopMode(BuildContext context) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (BuildContext routeContext) => DuoTabletopInjectionGuide(
          recommendedSite: recommendedSite,
          targetDoseMg: targetDoseMg,
          calculatedUnits: calculatedUnits,
          calculatedVolumeMl: calculatedVolumeMl,
          onConfirmInjection: () {
            Navigator.of(routeContext).pop();
            onConfirmInjection();
          },
          onClose: () => Navigator.of(routeContext).pop(),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override

    final bool isZh = context.locale.isZh;

    return Card(

        borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(18)),
      ),
      child: Padding(

/// ???詨?????嚗??犖????凝蝧閮??嚗?enum OnboardingPainPoint {
  muscleLoss(
    icon: Icons.fitness_center_rounded,
    titleZh: '????瘚仃???撘?,
    titleEn: 'Fear of muscle loss & loose skin',
    descZh: '?仿??芸??鞈芷?璅?扯??脫??凝蝧',
    descEn: 'Requires daily protein tracking to protect lean mass',
  ),
  unknownConcentration(
    icon: Icons.trending_up_rounded,
    titleZh: '銝?璆??批憭??交?????撜?,
    titleEn: 'Unclear active GLP-1 drug levels',
    descZh: '皜湔?????詨?嗥???※?※???暺?,
    descEn: 'Needs real-time Bateman pharmacokinetics curve',
  ),
  siteBruising(
    icon: Icons.accessibility_new_rounded,
    titleZh: '敹???芾ㄐ嚗??桃′憛???,
    titleEn: 'Injecting the same spot & bruising',
    descZh: '?閬?8 憭扯情????頛芣??????郎蝷?,
    descEn: 'Needs smart rotation map to prevent lipohypertrophy',
  ),

    icon: Icons.sick_rounded,

  const OnboardingPainPoint({
    required this.icon,

  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String titleZh;

  final String descZh;

  String title(AppLanguage lang) => lang.isZh ? titleZh : titleEn;

/// Onboarding ?Ｗ銋蝙?刻摨身摰???
@immutable
class OnboardingProfileData {
  const OnboardingProfileData({
    required this.medication,
    required this.currentDoseMg,
    required this.daysSinceLastShot,
    required this.startWeight,
    required this.targetWeight,
    required this.selectedPainPoints,
  });

  final Glp1Medication medication;
  final double currentDoseMg;
  final int daysSinceLastShot;
  final double startWeight;
  final double targetWeight;
  final Set<OnboardingPainPoint> selectedPainPoints;

  /// 靘??格?皜?撌株??其摯???望嚗誑?亙熒皜??? 0.75 kg/??隡啁?嚗?  int get estimatedWeeksToGoal {
    final double diff = (startWeight - targetWeight).abs();
    return math.max(4, (diff / 0.75).ceil());
  }
}

/// 擐活?? 5 甇仿?擃???Onboarding 閬?
class OnboardingView extends StatefulWidget {
  const OnboardingView({
    super.key,
    required this.onComplete,
    required this.onSkip,
  });

  final ValueChanged<OnboardingProfileData> onComplete;
  final VoidCallback onSkip;

  @override
  State<OnboardingView> createState() => _OnboardingViewState();
}

class _OnboardingViewState extends State<OnboardingView> {
  final PageController _pageController = PageController();
  int _currentStep = 0;

  // ???摮?  Glp1Medication _selectedMedication = Glp1Medication.tirzepatide;
  double _currentDoseMg = 2.5;
  int _daysSinceLastShot = 2;
  double _startWeight = 85.0;
  double _targetWeight = 70.0;
  final Set<OnboardingPainPoint> _selectedPainPoints = <OnboardingPainPoint>{
    OnboardingPainPoint.muscleLoss,
    OnboardingPainPoint.unknownConcentration,
  };

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _nextStep() {
    if (_currentStep < 4) {
      _pageController.nextPage(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 320),
        curve: Curves.easeInOutCubic,
      );
    } else {
      widget.onComplete(
        OnboardingProfileData(
          medication: _selectedMedication,
          currentDoseMg: _currentDoseMg,
          daysSinceLastShot: _daysSinceLastShot,
          startWeight: _startWeight,
          targetWeight: _targetWeight,
          selectedPainPoints: _selectedPainPoints,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  void _previousStep() {
    if (_currentStep > 0) {
      _pageController.previousPage(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
        curve: Curves.easeInOutCubic,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppStrings s = context.strings;
    final AppLanguage lang = context.locale.language;
    final WeightUnit unit = context.locale.weightUnit;
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: _currentStep > 0
            ? IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded, color: Color(0xFF0F172A)),
                onPressed: _previousStep,
              )
            : null,
        title: Row(
          children: List<Widget>.generate(5, (int index) {
            final bool isActive = index <= _currentStep;
            return Expanded(
              child: AnimatedContainer(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 240),
                height: 4.5,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2.5),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: isActive
                      ? const Color(0xFF0EA5E9)
                      : const Color(0xFFE2E8F0),
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(999)),
                ),
              ),
            );
          }),
        ),
        actions: <Widget>[
          TextButton(
            onPressed: widget.onSkip,
            child: Text(
              isZh ? '?仿?' : 'Skip',
              style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF64748B), fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
        ],
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: PageView(
          controller: _pageController,
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          onPageChanged: (int p) => setState(() => _currentStep = p),
          children: <Widget>[
            _buildStep1Medication(s, lang),
            _buildStep2DosageAndDate(s, lang),
            _buildStep3WeightGoal(s, lang, unit),
            _buildStep4PainPoints(s, lang),
            _buildStep5PlanRevealAndPaywall(s, lang, unit),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // Step 1: 頨怠??梢陷嚗迤?其蝙?冽?皞????芰車 GLP-1 ??嚗?  Widget _buildStep1Medication(AppStrings s, AppLanguage lang) {
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: <Widget>[
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(999)),
            ),
            child: Text(
              isZh ? '甇仿? 1 / 5 嚚???摰?' : 'Step 1 of 5 | Treatment Profile',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                color: Color(0xFF0284C7),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(
            isZh ? '?函?迤?其蝙?典蝔?GLP-1 ??嚗? : 'Which GLP-1 medication are you taking?',
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 22,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
              color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
              height: 1.3,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            isZh
                ? '蝟餌絞撠??府?亦??FDA 摰隞踹?嚗?函移蝣箸蝞??批?嗡??????文?銵唳?嚗?
                : 'DoseFlow calibrates your curve using official FDA pharmacokinetic half-life parameters:',
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: Color(0xFF64748B)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              children: Glp1Medication.values.map((Glp1Medication med) {
                final bool isSelected = med == _selectedMedication;
                return Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12.0),
                  child: InkWell(
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(16)),
                    onTap: () => setState(() => _selectedMedication = med),
                    child: AnimatedContainer(
                      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: isSelected
                            ? const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.08)
                            : Colors.white,
                        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(16)),
                        border: Border.all(
                          color: isSelected
                              ? const Color(0xFF0EA5E9)
                              : const Color(0xFFE2E8F0),
                          width: isSelected ? 2.0 : 1.2,
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: <Widget>[
                          Icon(
                            isSelected
                                ? Icons.radio_button_checked_rounded
                                : Icons.radio_button_off_rounded,
                            color: isSelected
                                ? const Color(0xFF0EA5E9)
                                : const Color(0xFF94A3B8),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 14),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: <Widget>[
                                Text(
                                  med.displayName(lang),
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 14.5,
                                    fontWeight: isSelected
                                        ? FontWeight.w800
                                        : FontWeight.w600,
                                    color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                Text(
                                  isZh
                                      ? '瘨?※??~${med.halfLifeHours.toInt()} 撠? 嚚??陸?? ~${med.peakHoursTmax.toInt()} 撠?'
                                      : 'Half-life ~${med.halfLifeHours.toInt()}h | Peak absorption ~${med.peakHoursTmax.toInt()}h',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 11.5,
                                    color: Color(0xFF64748B),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              }).toList(),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: _nextStep,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_rounded, size: 18),
              label: Text(isZh ? '銝?甇伐?閮剖??桀???' : 'Next: Set Current Dose'),
              style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(14)),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // Step 2: ?賣?????桀?????甈⊥??隞予?賂?蝡?刻??舀?蝞???嚗?  Widget _buildStep2DosageAndDate(AppStrings s, AppLanguage lang) {
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;
    const List<double> standardDoses = <double>[2.5, 5.0, 7.5, 10.0, 12.5, 15.0];
    final Color doseColor = PharmacokineticsEngine.colorForDose(_currentDoseMg);

    // ??單?瞍??嗅?擃畾???Aha Moment ?嚗?    final double activeResidualMg =
        PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
      doseMg: _currentDoseMg,
      medication: _selectedMedication,
      elapsedHours: _daysSinceLastShot * 24.0,
    );

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: <Widget>[
          Container(

  Widget _buildStep3WeightGoal(AppStrings s, AppLanguage lang, WeightUnit unit) {

        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (_startWeight * 2.20462) : _startWeight;

    final double weightDiff = (_startWeight - _targetWeight).clamp(0.0, 100.0);

        unit == WeightUnit.lbs ? (weightDiff * 2.20462) : weightDiff;
    final int estimatedWeeks = math.max(4, (weightDiff / 0.75).ceil());

        _startWeight > 0 ? (weightDiff / _startWeight * 100) : 0.0;

                isZh ? '?? 3 憭拙?鞎餉岫?其蒂?脣閮' : 'Start 3-Day Free Trial & View Plan',

              ),

          ),

  }

  Widget _buildSummaryBullet({
    required IconData icon,
    required Color color,
    required String title,
    required String desc,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10.0),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: <Widget>[
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(8)),
            ),
            child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 16),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: <Widget>[
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  desc,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Color(0xFF64748B)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// ?蝔?銝餃????????批?刻???????Onboarding 撠?頝舐
class DoseFlowApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const DoseFlowApp({super.key});

  @override
  State<DoseFlowApp> createState() => _DoseFlowAppState();
}

class _DoseFlowAppState extends State<DoseFlowApp> {
  late final LocaleController _localeController;
  late bool _hasCompletedOnboarding;
  OnboardingProfileData? _initialProfile;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _localeController = LocaleController(initialLanguage: AppLanguage.zhTW);

    final AppStorageSnapshot snapshot = AppPersistence.instance.current;
    _hasCompletedOnboarding =
        snapshot.logs.any((InjectionRecord r) => r.id != 'init_shot_1');
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _localeController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _handleOnboardingComplete(OnboardingProfileData data) async {
    HapticFeedbackService.milestoneHit();

    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    final AppStorageSnapshot newSnapshot = AppStorageSnapshot(
      logs: <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'onboarding_shot_${now.millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
          timestamp: now.subtract(Duration(days: data.daysSinceLastShot)),
          medication: data.medication,
          doseMg: data.currentDoseMg,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
          painLevel: 1,
        ),
      ],
      dailyProteinGrams: 45,
      targetProteinGrams: 90,
      dailyWaterMl: 1250,
      targetWaterMl: 2500,
      bowelCompletedToday: false,
      startWeightKg: data.startWeight,
      targetWeightKg: data.targetWeight,
      lastSavedTimestamp: now,
    );
    await AppPersistence.instance.saveSnapshot(newSnapshot);

    setState(() {
      _initialProfile = data;
      _hasCompletedOnboarding = true;
    });
  }

  void _handleSkipOnboarding() {
    HapticFeedbackService.lightImpact();
    setState(() {
      _hasCompletedOnboarding = true;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppLocaleScope(
      controller: _localeController,
      child: MaterialApp(
        title: 'DoseFlow / ?衣????Pro',
        debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
        theme: ThemeData(
          useMaterial3: true,
          colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
            seedColor: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9),
            brightness: Brightness.light,
          ),
          scaffoldBackgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
          appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
            backgroundColor: Colors.white,
            surfaceTintColor: Colors.transparent,
            elevation: 0,
            scrolledUnderElevation: 1.0,
          ),
          cardTheme: const CardThemeData(
            color: Colors.white,
            elevation: 0,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(18)),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        home: _hasCompletedOnboarding
            ? MainShellScreen(initialProfile: _initialProfile)
            : OnboardingView(
                onComplete: _handleOnboardingComplete,
                onSkip: _handleSkipOnboarding,
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 銝餅瑽撟??舀 iPhone Duo 7.6" ??撅?璅∪???4 Tabs 撠
class MainShellScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MainShellScreen({
    super.key,
    this.initialProfile,
  });

  final OnboardingProfileData? initialProfile;

  @override
  State<MainShellScreen> createState() => _MainShellScreenState();
}

class _MainShellScreenState extends State<MainShellScreen> {
  int _currentTabIndex = 0;

  late List<InjectionRecord> _logs;
  late List<DailySideEffectLog> _sideEffectLogs;

  late int _dailyProteinGrams;
  late int _targetProteinGrams;
  late int _dailyWaterMl;
  late int _targetWaterMl;
  late bool _bowelCompletedToday;
  late double _startWeightKg;
  late double _targetWeightKg;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    PurchaseService.instance.addListener(_onPurchaseStateChanged);

    final AppStorageSnapshot snapshot = AppPersistence.instance.current;
    _logs = List<InjectionRecord>.from(snapshot.logs);
    _dailyProteinGrams = snapshot.dailyProteinGrams;
    _targetProteinGrams = snapshot.targetProteinGrams;
    _dailyWaterMl = snapshot.dailyWaterMl;
    _targetWaterMl = snapshot.targetWaterMl;
    _bowelCompletedToday = snapshot.bowelCompletedToday;
    _startWeightKg = snapshot.startWeightKg;
    _targetWeightKg = snapshot.targetWeightKg;

    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    _sideEffectLogs = <DailySideEffectLog>[
      DailySideEffectLog(
        id: 'se_seed_1',
        date: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
        foodNoiseSuppression: 5,
        fatigueLevel: 2,
        nauseaLevel: 1,
      ),
      DailySideEffectLog(
        id: 'se_seed_2',
        date: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 2)),
        foodNoiseSuppression: 4,
        fatigueLevel: 3,
        nauseaLevel: 1,
      ),
      DailySideEffectLog(
        id: 'se_seed_3',
        date: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 3)),
        foodNoiseSuppression: 4,
        fatigueLevel: 1,
      ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    PurchaseService.instance.removeListener(_onPurchaseStateChanged);
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onPurchaseStateChanged() {
    setState(() {});
  }

  void _persistCurrentState() {
    final AppStorageSnapshot updated = AppStorageSnapshot(
      logs: _logs,
      dailyProteinGrams: _dailyProteinGrams,
      targetProteinGrams: _targetProteinGrams,
      dailyWaterMl: _dailyWaterMl,
      targetWaterMl: _targetWaterMl,
      bowelCompletedToday: _bowelCompletedToday,
      startWeightKg: _startWeightKg,
      targetWeightKg: _targetWeightKg,
      lastSavedTimestamp: DateTime.now(),
    );
    AppPersistence.instance.saveSnapshot(updated);
  }

  void _handleAddLog(InjectionRecord record) {
    HapticFeedbackService.heavyImpact();
    setState(() {
      _logs.insert(0, record);
    });
    _persistCurrentState();
  }

  void _handleTabSelected(int index) {
    if (_currentTabIndex != index) {
      HapticFeedbackService.selectionTick();
      setState(() => _currentTabIndex = index);
    }
  }

  void _handleOpenFoodNoiseDialog(BuildContext context) async {
    HapticFeedbackService.lightImpact();
    final DailySideEffectLog? log = await showDialog<DailySideEffectLog>(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext dialogContext) => const DailyFoodNoiseDialog(),
    );
    if (log != null && mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _sideEffectLogs.insert(0, log);
      });
      _persistCurrentState();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppStrings s = context.strings;
    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    final bool isPro = PurchaseService.instance.isProUnlocked;

    final double estimatedCurrentWeight = _logs.isNotEmpty
        ? (_startWeightKg - (_logs.length * 0.45)).clamp(_targetWeightKg, _startWeightKg)
        : _startWeightKg;

    final int? latestNoiseScore = _sideEffectLogs.isNotEmpty
        ? _sideEffectLogs.first.foodNoiseSuppression
        : null;

    final Widget dashboardPage = HomeDashboardView(
      logs: _logs,
      now: now,
      isProUnlocked: isPro,
      onToggleProPreview: PurchaseService.instance.toggleProForTesting,
      proteinGrams: _dailyProteinGrams,
      targetProteinGrams: _targetProteinGrams,
      waterMl: _dailyWaterMl,
      targetWaterMl: _targetWaterMl,
      bowelCompleted: _bowelCompletedToday,
      latestFoodNoiseScore: latestNoiseScore,
      onOpenFoodNoiseDialog: () => _handleOpenFoodNoiseDialog(context),
      onQuickLogShot: _handleAddLog,
      onAddProtein: (int g) {
        HapticFeedbackService.selectionTick();
        setState(() => _dailyProteinGrams += g);
        _persistCurrentState();
      },
      onAddWater: (int ml) {
        HapticFeedbackService.selectionTick();
        setState(() => _dailyWaterMl += ml);
        _persistCurrentState();
      },
      onToggleBowel: () {
        HapticFeedbackService.successPattern();
        setState(() => _bowelCompletedToday = !_bowelCompletedToday);
        _persistCurrentState();
      },
    );

    final Widget analyticsPage = AnalyticsView(
      logs: _logs,
      sideEffectLogs: _sideEffectLogs,
      startWeightKg: _startWeightKg,
      currentWeightKg: estimatedCurrentWeight,
      isProUnlocked: isPro,
      onRequestUnlockPro: PurchaseService.instance.toggleProForTesting,
    );

    final List<Widget> pages = <Widget>[
      dashboardPage,
      ShotLogAndCalculatorView(
        logs: _logs,
        isProUnlocked: isPro,
        onAddLog: _handleAddLog,
        onRequestUnlockPro: PurchaseService.instance.toggleProForTesting,
      ),
      analyticsPage,
      SettingsAndPaywallView(
        isProUnlocked: isPro,
        onTogglePro: PurchaseService.instance.toggleProForTesting,
      ),
    ];

    final PreferredSizeWidget topAppBar = AppBar(
      title: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: <Widget>[
          Text(
            s.appTitle,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 16.5,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
              color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            s.appSubtitle,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 11.0,
              color: Color(0xFF64748B),
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      actions: const <Widget>[
        Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(right: 12.0),
          child: LanguageTogglePill(),
        ),
      ],
    );

    final Widget bottomNav = NavigationBar(
      selectedIndex: _currentTabIndex,
      onDestinationSelected: _handleTabSelected,
      destinations: <NavigationDestination>[
        NavigationDestination(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),
          selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.dashboard_rounded),
          label: s.tabDashboard,
        ),
        NavigationDestination(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.colorize_outlined),
          selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.colorize_rounded),
          label: s.tabShotLog,
        ),
        NavigationDestination(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.insights_outlined),
          selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.insights_rounded),
          label: s.tabAnalytics,
        ),
        NavigationDestination(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.tune_outlined),
          selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.tune_rounded),
          label: s.tabSettings,
        ),
      ],
    );

    // iPhone Duo 7.6" 撅?璅∪?嚗????蝝?椰甈?銵冽 嚗??單???頞典??甈蒂??    if (_currentTabIndex == 0 && DuoDeviceDetector.isLargeScreen(context)) {
      return DuoAdaptiveScaffold(
        appBar: topAppBar,
        primaryPane: dashboardPage,
        secondaryPane: analyticsPage,
        bottomNavigationBar: bottomNav,
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: topAppBar,
      body: IndexedStack(
        index: _currentTabIndex,
        children: pages,
      ),
      bottomNavigationBar: bottomNav,
    );
  }
}

/// Tab 1: 隞?銵冽 (?游? iPhone Duo ???質???～?摨行蝺?敺桃???
class HomeDashboardView extends StatelessWidget {
  const HomeDashboardView({
    super.key,
    required this.logs,
    required this.now,
    required this.isProUnlocked,
    required this.onToggleProPreview,
    required this.proteinGrams,
    required this.targetProteinGrams,
    required this.waterMl,
    required this.targetWaterMl,
    required this.bowelCompleted,
    required this.latestFoodNoiseScore,
    required this.onOpenFoodNoiseDialog,
    required this.onQuickLogShot,
    required this.onAddProtein,
    required this.onAddWater,
    required this.onToggleBowel,
  });

  final List<InjectionRecord> logs;
  final DateTime now;
  final bool isProUnlocked;
  final VoidCallback onToggleProPreview;
  final int proteinGrams;
  final int targetProteinGrams;
  final int waterMl;
  final int targetWaterMl;
  final bool bowelCompleted;
  final int? latestFoodNoiseScore;
  final VoidCallback onOpenFoodNoiseDialog;
  final ValueChanged<InjectionRecord> onQuickLogShot;
  final ValueChanged<int> onAddProtein;
  final ValueChanged<int> onAddWater;
  final VoidCallback onToggleBowel;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppStrings s = context.strings;
    final AppLanguage lang = context.locale.language;
    final bool isZh = lang.isZh;

    int daysLeft = 4;
    int hoursLeft = 18;
    if (logs.isNotEmpty) {
      final DateTime nextShotTime =
          logs.first.timestamp.add(const Duration(days: 7));
      final Duration diff = nextShotTime.difference(now);
      if (diff.isNegative) {
        daysLeft = 0;
        hoursLeft = 0;
      } else {
        daysLeft = diff.inDays;
        hoursLeft = diff.inHours % 24;
      }
    }

    final InjectionSite nextSite =
        PharmacokineticsEngine.recommendNextSite(logs);
    final double activeDose = logs.isNotEmpty ? logs.first.doseMg : 2.5;
    final Glp1Medication activeMed =
        logs.isNotEmpty ? logs.first.medication : Glp1Medication.tirzepatide;

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      children: <Widget>[
        // 1. ???嚗 嚗?撱箄降銝活?賣??其?
        Card(
          elevation: 0,
          color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.08),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(18)),
            side: BorderSide(
              color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.28),
            ),
          ),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
            child: Row(
              children: <Widget>[
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    color: Color(0xFF0EA5E9),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(14)),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(
                    Icons.alarm_on_rounded,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    size: 24,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: <Widget>[
                      Text(
                        s.nextShotCountdownTitle,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12.0,
                          color: Color(0xFF475569),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        s.formatDaysHours(daysLeft, hoursLeft),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 18.0,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                          color: Color(0xFF0F172A),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        '${s.recommendedNextSiteLabel}嚗?{nextSite.label(lang)}',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          color: Color(0xFF0284C7),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),

        // 2. iPhone Duo 撠惇嚗?0 摨血????Ｗ???亙?撘??
        DuoOuterQuickActionCard(
          recommendedSite: nextSite,
          targetDoseMg: activeDose,
          calculatedUnits: 50.0,
          calculatedVolumeMl: 0.50,
          onConfirmInjection: () {
            onQuickLogShot(
              InjectionRecord(
                id: 'duo_shot_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
                timestamp: DateTime.now(),
                medication: activeMed,
                doseMg: activeDose,
                site: nextSite,
                painLevel: 1,
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),

        // 3. ?詨?甇血嚗??扯?拇?摨血?蝺??        DoseConcentrationCard(
          logs: logs,
          now: now,
          isProUnlocked: isProUnlocked,
          onToggleProPreview: onToggleProPreview,
          onOpenSwiftCodeDialog: () =>
              SwiftReferenceExporter.showSwiftCodeModal(context),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),

        // 4. ?脫??凝蝧???暸??單??∪??        Card(
          elevation: 0,
          color: Colors.white,
          shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(20)),
            side: BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFE2E8F0)),
          ),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: <Widget>[
                Row(
                  children: <Widget>[
                    const Icon(
                      Icons.fitness_center_rounded,
                      color: Color(0xFFF97316),
                      size: 20,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),

                _buildHabitRow(
