                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    OutlinedButton(
                      onPressed: () => onAddWater(500),
                      child: Text(s.waterQuickAdd500, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const Divider(height: 24),
                InkWell(
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                  onTap: onToggleBowel,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: bowelCompleted
                          ? const Color(0xFF10B981).withValues(alpha: 0.12)
                          : const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
                      borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: bowelCompleted
                            ? const Color(0xFF10B981)
                            : const Color(0xFFCBD5E1),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: <Widget>[
                        Icon(
                          bowelCompleted
                              ? Icons.check_circle_rounded
                              : Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded,
                          color: bowelCompleted
                              ? const Color(0xFF10B981)
                              : const Color(0xFF64748B),
                          size: 22,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 10),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            bowelCompleted
                                ? s.bowelCompletedText
                                : s.bowelPendingText,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 12.5,
                              fontWeight: bowelCompleted
                                  ? FontWeight.w700
                                  : FontWeight.w500,
                              color: bowelCompleted
                                  ? const Color(0xFF065F46)
                                  : const Color(0xFF334155),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                InkWell(
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                  onTap: onOpenFoodNoiseDialog,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                      borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
                      border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9).withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: <Widget>[
                        const Icon(Icons.psychology_rounded, color: Color(0xFF0EA5E9), size: 22),
                        const SizedBox(width: 10),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            latestFoodNoiseScore != null
                                ? (isZh
                                    ? '隞憭扯憌?嚗?latestFoodNoiseScore ??(暺??閰?)'
                                    : 'Today\'s Food Noise: $latestFoodNoiseScore/5 (Tap to edit)')
                                : (isZh
                                    ? '暺?閮?隞憭扯憌? (10蝘翰????'
                                    : 'Tap to log today\'s Food Noise (10s check-in)'),
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 12.5,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                              color: Color(0xFF0369A1),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded, size: 14, color: Color(0xFF0EA5E9)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHabitRow({
    required String title,
    required double progress,
    required Color color,
    required List<Widget> buttons,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: <Widget>[
        Text(
          title,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12.5,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            color: Color(0xFF334155),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        LinearProgressIndicator(
          value: progress,
          backgroundColor: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
          valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(color),
          minHeight: 8,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(4)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
          children: buttons,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 擃??????臬?璅??璅∪?
@immutable
class WeightPoint {
  const WeightPoint({
    required this.date,
    required this.weightKg,
    required this.associatedDoseMg,
  });

  final DateTime date;
  final double weightKg;
  final double associatedDoseMg;

  double displayWeight(WeightUnit unit) {
    if (unit == WeightUnit.lbs) {
      return double.parse((weightKg * 2.20462).toStringAsFixed(1));
    }
    return weightKg;
  }
}

/// ???擃????鼓鋆賢 (Dose-Colored Weight Chart)
class DoseColoredWeightChartPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const DoseColoredWeightChartPainter({
    required this.points,
    required this.isProUnlocked,
    required this.unit,
  });

  final List<WeightPoint> points;
  final bool isProUnlocked;
  final WeightUnit unit;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    if (points.length < 2) return;

    const double leftPad = 38.0;
    const double rightPad = 24.0;
    const double topPad = 24.0;
    const double bottomPad = 28.0;
    final double plotWidth = size.width - leftPad - rightPad;
    final double plotHeight = size.height - topPad - bottomPad;

    final List<double> vals =
        points.map((WeightPoint p) => p.displayWeight(unit)).toList();
    final double minVal = vals.reduce(math.min) - 1.5;
    final double maxVal = vals.reduce(math.max) + 1.5;
    final double valRange = math.max(1.0, maxVal - minVal);

    // 1. 蝜芾ˊ瘞游像?潛???Y 頠詨摨行??    final Paint gridPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFFCBD5E1).withValues(alpha: 0.35)
      ..strokeWidth = 1.0;

    for (int i = 0; i <= 3; i++) {
      final double y = topPad + plotHeight * (i / 3.0);
      final double tickVal = maxVal - (valRange * (i / 3.0));
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(leftPad, y), Offset(size.width - rightPad, y), gridPaint);

      final TextPainter tp = TextPainter(
        text: TextSpan(
          text: tickVal.toStringAsFixed(0),
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 10.0,
            color: Color(0xFF64748B),
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
        textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
      )..layout();
      tp.paint(canvas, Offset(leftPad - tp.width - 6, y - tp.height / 2));
    }

    Offset getCoord(int i) {
      final double x = leftPad + (i / (points.length - 1)) * plotWidth;
      final double y =
          topPad + plotHeight * (1.0 - (vals[i] - minVal) / valRange);
      return Offset(x, y);
    }

    // 2. 蝜芾ˊ??畾蛛?Pro 閫??靘????莎??祥?鼓鋆賢銝?啗??嚗?    for (int i = 0; i < points.length - 1; i++) {
      final Offset p1 = getCoord(i);
      final Offset p2 = getCoord(i + 1);
      final Color segmentColor = isProUnlocked
          ? PharmacokineticsEngine.colorForDose(points[i + 1].associatedDoseMg)
          : const Color(0xFF94A3B8);

      final Paint linePaint = Paint()
        ..color = segmentColor
        ..strokeWidth = 3.2
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;
      canvas.drawLine(p1, p2, linePaint);
    }

    // 3. 蝜芾ˊ蝭暺????詨潭?蝐?    for (int i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
      final Offset pt = getCoord(i);
      final Color nodeColor = isProUnlocked
          ? PharmacokineticsEngine.colorForDose(points[i].associatedDoseMg)
          : const Color(0xFF64748B);

      canvas.drawCircle(
        pt,
        5.5,
        Paint()..color = Colors.white,
      );
      canvas.drawCircle(
        pt,
        4.0,
        Paint()..color = nodeColor,
      );

      final TextPainter tp = TextPainter(
        text: TextSpan(
          text: vals[i].toStringAsFixed(1),
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 9.5,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            color: nodeColor,
          ),
        ),
        textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
      )..layout();
      tp.paint(canvas, Offset(pt.dx - tp.width / 2, pt.dy - 16));
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant DoseColoredWeightChartPainter oldDelegate) {
    return oldDelegate.points != points ||
        oldDelegate.isProUnlocked != isProUnlocked ||
        oldDelegate.unit != unit;
  }
}

  void _showDoctorExportDialog(BuildContext context) {
    HapticFeedbackService.mediumImpact();
    showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext dialogContext) {
        return ClinicalExportDialog(
          logs: logs,
          startWeightKg: startWeightKg,
          currentWeightKg: currentWeightKg,
        );
      },

  static Widget _buildStatColumn({
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return Column(
      children: <Widget>[
        Text(
          label,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Color(0xFF64748B)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          value,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16.0,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
            color: color,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static Widget _buildSideEffectRow({
    required String dayLabel,
    required double foodNoiseLevel,
    required double symptomLevel,
    required String noiseText,
    required String symptomText,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: <Widget>[
        Text(
          dayLabel,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.0, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Row(
          children: <Widget>[
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: <Widget>[
                  Text(
                    noiseText,
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: Color(0xFF10B981)),
                  ),
                  LinearProgressIndicator(
                    value: foodNoiseLevel.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                    backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF10B981).withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Color(0xFF10B981)),
                    minHeight: 6,
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(3)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: <Widget>[
                  Text(
                    symptomText,
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: Color(0xFFF97316)),
                  ),
                  LinearProgressIndicator(
                    value: symptomLevel.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                    backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF97316).withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Color(0xFFF97316)),
                    minHeight: 6,
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(3)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Tab 4: 獢撠極?琿?閬賬???ASO ??瑼Ｚ??具璈?隞質?隞祥?身摰?class SettingsAndPaywallView extends StatelessWidget {

  void _showBackupDialog(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isZh = context.locale.isZh;
    final String backupBase64 = AppPersistence.instance.exportBackupString();

    showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext dialogContext) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: Text(isZh ? '?臬?祆??券??遢' : 'Export Local Backup'),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: <Widget>[
              Text(
                isZh
                    ? '隞乩??箏?撖?銝莎???冽????賣?蝝?凝蝧?脣漲???風?莎?'
                    : 'Encrypted Base64 string containing all logs, micro-habits and weight history:',
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Color(0xFF475569)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(8)),
                  border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFCBD5E1)),
                ),
                child: SelectableText(
                  backupBase64,
                  maxLines: 4,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'Consolas'),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: <Widget>[
            TextButton.icon(
              onPressed: () {
                Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: backupBase64));
                HapticFeedbackService.successPattern();
                Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop();
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text(
                      isZh
                          ? '?? 撌脰?鋆賢?隞賢?銝脰?芾票蝪選??臬戎???潛?鈭箏?敹???
                          : '?? Copied backup string to clipboard!',
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 16),
              label: Text(isZh ? '銴ˊ?遢摮葡' : 'Copy String'),
            ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(),
              child: Text(isZh ? '??' : 'Close'),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showRestoreDialog(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isZh = context.locale.isZh;
    final TextEditingController textController = TextEditingController();

    showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext dialogContext) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: Text(isZh ? '鞎潔????遢' : 'Restore from Backup String'),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: <Widget>[
              TextField(
                controller: textController,
                maxLines: 4,
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: isZh ? '?冽迨鞎潔??遢 Base64 摮葡...' : 'Paste Base64 backup string here...',
                  border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                ),
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'Consolas'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: <Widget>[
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () async {
                final String pasted = textController.text.trim();
                final bool success =
                    await AppPersistence.instance.importBackupString(pasted);
                if (dialogContext.mounted) {
                  Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop();
                }
                if (success) {
                  HapticFeedbackService.successPattern();
                  if (context.mounted) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                      SnackBar(
                        content: Text(
                          isZh
                              ? '?? ?遢????嚗?唳?歇?郊?湔??
                              : '?? Backup restored successfully!',
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }
                } else {
                  HapticFeedbackService.heavyImpact();
                  if (context.mounted) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                      SnackBar(
                        content: Text(
                          isZh
                              ? '?? ?遢?澆??⊥??歇??嚗?蝣箄?摮葡摰?扼?
                              : '?? Invalid or corrupted backup format.',
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }
                }
              },
              child: Text(isZh ? '蝣箄???' : 'Confirm Restore'),
            ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(),
              child: Text(isZh ? '??' : 'Cancel'),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showPaywallModal(BuildContext context) {
    final AppStrings s = context.strings;
    final bool isZh = context.locale.isZh;

    showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(28)),
      ),
      builder: (BuildContext sheetContext) {
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 24, 20, 32),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: <Widget>[
              Center(
                child: Container(
                  width: 42,
                  height: 4,

                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      s.paywallHeadline,
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),

class WebFunnelApp extends StatelessWidget {

class MaxWidthContainer extends StatelessWidget {
  const MaxWidthContainer({super.key, required this.child});
  final Widget child;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520),
        child: child,
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== test/clinical_export_test.dart ===

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/app_localization.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/clinical_export_service.dart';

void main() {
  group('Clinical Adherence & Velocity Metrics Calculation Tests', () {
    test('Empty logs baseline produces safe 100% default metrics with zero crashes', () {
      final ClinicalAdherenceMetrics metrics = ClinicalExportService.calculateMetrics(
        logs: <InjectionRecord>[],
        startWeightKg: 85.0,
        currentWeightKg: 80.0,
      );

      expect(metrics.adherenceRatePercent, equals(100.0));
      expect(metrics.siteRotationCompliancePercent, equals(100.0));
      expect(metrics.weeklyWeightVelocityKg, equals(0.0));
      expect(metrics.totalLossKg, equals(0.0));
      expect(metrics.totalLogsCount, equals(0));
      expect(metrics.distinctSitesUsed, equals(0));
    });

    test('Regular 7-day titration with distinct sites yields 100% adherence and rotation', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_1',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 35)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_2',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 28)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_3',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 21)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftUpperAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_4',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 14)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.rightUpperAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_5',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.leftThigh,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_6',
          timestamp: now,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.rightThigh,
        ),
      ];

      final ClinicalAdherenceMetrics metrics = ClinicalExportService.calculateMetrics(
        logs: logs,
        startWeightKg: 86.5,
        currentWeightKg: 81.6,
      );

      expect(metrics.adherenceRatePercent, equals(100.0));
      expect(metrics.siteRotationCompliancePercent, equals(100.0));
      expect(metrics.distinctSitesUsed, equals(6));
      expect(metrics.totalLogsCount, equals(6));
      expect(metrics.totalLossKg, equals(4.9));
      expect(metrics.weeklyWeightVelocityKg, greaterThan(0.7));
    });
  });

  group('Adherence Violation & Non-compliant Rotation Boundary Tests', () {
    test('Delayed and missed shots properly penalize adherence rate percentage', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      // 6 ???5 ????3 ?迤撣?7憭?嚗? ??辣??14憭?嚗? ?辣??12憭?
      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'v_1',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 47)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'v_2',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 40)), // 7 憭?(?)
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'v_3',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 33)), // 7 憭?(?)
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftUpperAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'v_4',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 19)), // 14 憭?(瞍???!)
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.rightUpperAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'v_5',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)), // 12 憭?(撱園??!)
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.leftThigh,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'v_6',
          timestamp: now, // 7 憭?(?)
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.rightThigh,
        ),
      ];

      final ClinicalAdherenceMetrics metrics = ClinicalExportService.calculateMetrics(
        logs: logs,
        startWeightKg: 85.0,
        currentWeightKg: 81.0,
      );

      // 5 ???葉??3 ??閬?-> 3 / 5 = 60.0%
      expect(metrics.adherenceRatePercent, equals(60.0));
      expect(metrics.siteRotationCompliancePercent, equals(100.0));
    });

    test('Repeated consecutive site injections triggers lipohypertrophy rotation penalties', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      // 4 ???3 ????2 甈⊿???賣??雿?閬?1 甈∪???閬?      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'r_1',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 21)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen,
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'r_2',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 14)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen, // ????!
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'r_3',
          timestamp: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen, // ????!
        ),
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'r_4',
          timestamp: now,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen, // ????
        ),
      ];

      final ClinicalAdherenceMetrics metrics = ClinicalExportService.calculateMetrics(
        logs: logs,
        startWeightKg: 85.0,
        currentWeightKg: 82.5,
      );

      // 3 甈∟?蝘颱葉??1 甈∪?閬?-> 1 / 3 = 33.3%
      expect(metrics.siteRotationCompliancePercent, equals(33.3));
      expect(metrics.distinctSitesUsed, equals(2));
    });
  });

  group('RFC 4180 CSV & Physician HTML Formatting Tests', () {
    test('generateCsv adheres strictly to RFC 4180 with proper quotation and escaping', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'c_1',
          timestamp: now,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen,
          painLevel: 1,
          batchOrVialNote: 'Batch #12, "Reconstituted" safely',
        ),
      ];

      // 1. 蝜?銝剜? CSV 皜祈岫
      final String csvZh = ClinicalExportService.generateCsv(
        logs: logs,
        lang: AppLanguage.zhTW,
      );
      expect(csvZh, contains('?賣?摨?,?交?,??,?亦??,?賣???(mg),瘜典??其?,?潛???0-5),?酉/?亦?寡?'));
      // 撽?????撘?甈?頧儔 (RFC 4180 閬?)
      expect(csvZh, contains('"Batch #12, ""Reconstituted"" safely"'));

      // 2. ?望? CSV 皜祈岫
      final String csvEn = ClinicalExportService.generateCsv(
        logs: logs,
        lang: AppLanguage.enUS,
      );
      expect(csvEn, contains('ShotIndex,Date,Time,Medication,Dose(mg),InjectionSite,PainLevel(0-5),BatchOrNotes'));
      expect(csvEn, contains('Tirzepatide'));
    });

    test('generatePhysicianSummaryHtml outputs print-optimized A4 layout and B2B2C referral watermark', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'p_1',
          timestamp: now,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
          painLevel: 1,
        ),
      ];

      for (final AppLanguage lang in AppLanguage.values) {
        final String html = ClinicalExportService.generatePhysicianSummaryHtml(
          logs: logs,
          startWeightKg: 85.0,
          currentWeightKg: 81.2,
          lang: lang,
          unit: WeightUnit.kg,
        );

        expect(html, contains('<!DOCTYPE html>'));
        expect(html, contains('@page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 12mm; }'));
        expect(html, contains('@media print'));
        expect(html, contains('https://doseflow.app'));
        expect(html, contains('Clinician Partner Ready'));
        expect(html, contains('5.0 mg'));
      }
    });

    test('generatePlainTextSummary formats clean medical progress note for clinic messaging', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'txt_1',
          timestamp: now,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen,
        ),
      ];

      for (final AppLanguage lang in AppLanguage.values) {
        final String summary = ClinicalExportService.generatePlainTextSummary(
          logs: logs,
          startWeightKg: 85.0,
          currentWeightKg: 82.0,
          lang: lang,
          unit: WeightUnit.kg,
        );

        expect(summary, isNotEmpty);
        if (lang.isZh) {
          expect(summary, contains('?oseFlow ?衣??閮箄摨脣漲?勗???));
          expect(summary, contains('蝝航?皜? -3.0 kg'));
        } else {
          expect(summary, contains('[DoseFlow Clinical Visit Summary]'));
          expect(summary, contains('Total Loss: -3.0 kg'));
        }
      }
    });
  });
}


=== test/compliance_audit_test.dart ===

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/app_localization.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/legal_compliance_hub.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/purchase_service.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/app_persistence.dart';

void main() {
  group('Apple Review Guideline 1.4.1 Medical Safety & FDA Citations Audit', () {
    test('All GLP-1 medications must have verified official FDA Prescribing Info URLs', () {
      for (final Glp1Medication med in Glp1Medication.values) {
        expect(med.fdaCitationTitle, isNotEmpty);
        expect(med.fdaCitationUrl, startsWith('https://'));
        expect(med.fdaCitationUrl.endsWith('.pdf'), isTrue);
        expect(
          med.fdaCitationUrl.contains('lilly.com') ||
              med.fdaCitationUrl.contains('novo-pi.com'),
          isTrue,
        );
      }
    });

    test('Medical disclaimer explicitly disclaims diagnosis and dosage titration', () {
      for (final AppLanguage lang in AppLanguage.values) {
        final String disclaimer =
            LegalComplianceHub.getMedicalDisclaimerStatement(lang);
        expect(disclaimer, isNotEmpty);
        if (lang.isZh) {
          expect(disclaimer.contains('????), isTrue);
          expect(disclaimer.contains('蝯???隞颱?????'), isTrue);
          expect(disclaimer.contains('FDA'), isTrue);
        } else {
          expect(disclaimer.contains('NOT a medical device'), isTrue);
          expect(disclaimer.contains('NEVER recommends'), isTrue);
          expect(disclaimer.contains('FDA'), isTrue);
        }
      }
    });

    test('Pharmacokinetics engine boundary stability: zero NaN or Infinity under extreme inputs', () {
      for (final Glp1Medication med in Glp1Medication.values) {
        // ?? 1: ?? <= 0
        expect(
          PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
            doseMg: 2.5,
            medication: med,
            elapsedHours: -1.0,
          ),
          equals(0.0),
        );

        // ?? 2: 頞? 60 憭拐誨雓?蝒?        expect(
          PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
            doseMg: 2.5,
            medication: med,
            elapsedHours: 1500.0,
          ),
          equals(0.0),
        );

        // ?? 3: 甇?虜?典?皛游?????嚗?澆?????憭扳 0
        for (final double dose in <double>[2.5, 5.0, 7.5, 10.0, 15.0]) {
          final double conc = PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
            doseMg: dose,
            medication: med,
            elapsedHours: med.peakHoursTmax,
          );
          expect(conc.isFinite, isTrue);
          expect(conc, greaterThan(0.0));
        }
      }
    });
  });

  group('Apple Review Guideline 5.1.1 & Local-First Privacy Audit', () {
    test('Privacy Policy HTML contains required HealthKit and Local-First disclosures', () {
      for (final AppLanguage lang in AppLanguage.values) {
        final String html = LegalComplianceHub.generatePrivacyPolicyHtml(lang);
        expect(html, isNotEmpty);
        expect(html.contains('<!DOCTYPE html>'), isTrue);

        if (lang.isZh) {
          expect(html.contains('100% ?砍蝡舀瑽??嗡撩?摮'), isTrue);
          expect(html.contains('Apple HealthKit ?豢???隤芣?'), isTrue);
          expect(html.contains('?嗥洵銝餈質馱'), isTrue);
        } else {
          expect(html.contains('100% Local-First Architecture'), isTrue);
          expect(html.contains('Apple HealthKit Integration'), isTrue);
          expect(html.contains('Zero Third-Party Trackers'), isTrue);
        }
      }
    });

    test('Guardrail 09 Strict Base64 validation: rejects malformed and non-standard strings', () {
      // 1. ?? Base64 摮葡撽? (?瑕漲??4 ?銝???皞?
      const String validSample1 = 'eyJzY2hlbWFWZXJzaW9uIjoxfQ==';
      const String validSample2 = 'dGVzdA==';
      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64(validSample1), isTrue);
      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64(validSample2), isTrue);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64(validSample1), isTrue);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64(validSample2), isTrue);

      // 2. ?瑕漲??4 ???潭???      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64('abc'), isFalse);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64('abc'), isFalse);

      // 3. ?摨???????皞?Base64 摮泵嚗??湔?
      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64('dGVz_dA=='), isFalse);
      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64('dGVz-dA=='), isFalse);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64('dGVz_dA=='), isFalse);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64('dGVz-dA=='), isFalse);

      // 4. 蝛箏潭?蝛箇??潭???      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64(''), isFalse);
      expect(AppPersistence.isValidBackupBase64(null), isFalse);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64(''), isFalse);
      expect(ReceiptValidator.isValidBase64(null), isFalse);
    });
  });

  group('StoreKit 2 IAP Metadata & Guardrail 10 Alignment Audit', () {
    test('DoseFlowProductCatalog aligns with StoreKit 2 specification and pricing tiers', () {
      expect(SubscriptionTier.monthly.productId, equals('com.doseflow.app.monthly'));
      expect(SubscriptionTier.annual.productId, equals('com.doseflow.app.annual'));
      expect(SubscriptionTier.lifetime.productId, equals('com.doseflow.app.lifetime'));

      final ProductItem annual = DoseFlowProductCatalog.getByTier(SubscriptionTier.annual);
      expect(annual.isBestValue, isTrue);
      expect(annual.hasFreeTrial, isTrue);
      expect(annual.trialDays, equals(3));
      expect(annual.priceUsdString, equals(r'$39.99'));
      expect(annual.priceTwdString, equals(r'NT$ 990'));

      final ProductItem monthly = DoseFlowProductCatalog.getByTier(SubscriptionTier.monthly);
      expect(monthly.hasFreeTrial, isFalse);
      expect(monthly.priceUsdString, equals(r'$6.99'));

      final ProductItem lifetime = DoseFlowProductCatalog.getByTier(SubscriptionTier.lifetime);
      expect(lifetime.hasFreeTrial, isFalse);
      expect(lifetime.priceUsdString, equals(r'$79.99'));
    });

    test('Guardrail 10 Compliance: All IAP descriptions and titles truncated within 45 characters', () {
      for (final ProductItem p in DoseFlowProductCatalog.all) {
        expect(p.titleZh.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(45));
        expect(p.titleEn.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(45));
      }

      final String reviewNotes =
          LegalComplianceHub.generateAppStoreReviewNotes(AppLanguage.enUS);
      expect(reviewNotes, isNotEmpty);
      expect(reviewNotes.contains('GUIDELINE 1.4.1'), isTrue);
      expect(reviewNotes.contains('GUIDELINE 5.1.1'), isTrue);
      expect(reviewNotes.contains('IN-APP PURCHASE & SUBSCRIPTION TESTING'), isTrue);
    });
  });
}


=== test/food_noise_test.dart ===

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/app_localization.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/food_noise_engine.dart';

void main() {
  group('Food Noise & Side Effect Modeling Tests', () {
    test('DailySideEffectLog clamps boundaries and enforces safe defaults', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();

      // 撽?頞??芸??嗆??喳?瘜???      final Map<String, dynamic> rawMap = <String, dynamic>{
        'id': 'test_se_1',
        'date': now.toIso8601String(),
        'foodNoiseSuppression': 10, // 頞??? 5
        'nauseaLevel': -2, // 鞎?? 0
        'fatigueLevel': 8, // 頞??? 5
        'acidRefluxLevel': 3,
        'constipationLevel': 2,
        'notes': 'Slight nausea after meal',
      };

      final DailySideEffectLog log = DailySideEffectLog.fromMap(rawMap);
      expect(log.foodNoiseSuppression, equals(5));
      expect(log.nauseaLevel, equals(0));
      expect(log.fatigueLevel, equals(5));
      expect(log.acidRefluxLevel, equals(3));
      expect(log.constipationLevel, equals(2));
      expect(log.notes, equals('Slight nausea after meal'));
    });

    test('overallSymptomSeverity calculates weighted clinical index correctly', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      // ?擃?????fatigueLevel = 4 (甈? 70%)
      // ??撟喳???(1 + 4 + 1 + 2) / 4.0 = 2.0 (甈? 30%)
      // 蝬?閰???4 * 0.7 + 2.0 * 0.3 = 2.8 + 0.6 = 3.4
      final DailySideEffectLog log = DailySideEffectLog(
        id: 'test_se_2',
        date: now,
        foodNoiseSuppression: 4,
        nauseaLevel: 1,
        fatigueLevel: 4,
        acidRefluxLevel: 1,
        constipationLevel: 2,
      );

      expect(log.overallSymptomSeverity, equals(3.4));
    });

    test('DailySideEffectLog serialization roundtrip preserves all values', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final DailySideEffectLog original = DailySideEffectLog(
        id: 'roundtrip_se',
        date: now,
        foodNoiseSuppression: 3,
        nauseaLevel: 2,
        fatigueLevel: 3,
        acidRefluxLevel: 0,
        constipationLevel: 4,
        notes: 'Constipation managed with water',
      );

      final Map<String, dynamic> map = original.toMap();
      final DailySideEffectLog restored = DailySideEffectLog.fromMap(map);

      expect(restored.id, equals(original.id));
      expect(restored.foodNoiseSuppression, equals(original.foodNoiseSuppression));
      expect(restored.nauseaLevel, equals(original.nauseaLevel));
      expect(restored.fatigueLevel, equals(original.fatigueLevel));
      expect(restored.acidRefluxLevel, equals(original.acidRefluxLevel));
      expect(restored.constipationLevel, equals(original.constipationLevel));
      expect(restored.notes, equals(original.notes));
    });
  });

  group('FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine 7-Day Profile Analytics Tests', () {
    test('Cold-start sparse fallback generates standard Bateman clinical curve', () {
      final List<DayCorrelationProfile> profiles =
          FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine.calculate7DayProfile(
        sideEffectLogs: <DailySideEffectLog>[],
        injectionLogs: <InjectionRecord>[],
      );

      expect(profiles.length, equals(7));
      // 撽? Day 1 ?陸憯 (4.8) 憭扳 Day 7 ?※???(1.9)
      expect(profiles.first.postShotDay, equals(1));
      expect(profiles.first.avgFoodNoiseSuppression, equals(4.8));
      expect(profiles.last.postShotDay, equals(7));
      expect(profiles.last.avgFoodNoiseSuppression, equals(1.9));
      expect(profiles.first.avgFoodNoiseSuppression,
          greaterThan(profiles.last.avgFoodNoiseSuppression));
    });

    test('Correlating actual logs into 7-day post-shot buckets averages accurately', () {
      final DateTime shotTime = DateTime(2026, 11, 1, 8, 0);
      final List<InjectionRecord> shots = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_base',
          timestamp: shotTime,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 5.0,
          site: InjectionSite.rightLowerAbdomen,
        ),
      ];

      // 瘜典 Day 1 (?賣?敺?6 撠???12 撠?) ?拍?蝝??? 5 ??3 -> 撟喳?? 4.0
      // 瘜典 Day 2 (?賣?敺?30 撠?) 銝蝑????脣? 4 -> Day 2 ?脣?? 4.0
      final List<DailySideEffectLog> seLogs = <DailySideEffectLog>[
        DailySideEffectLog(
          id: 'se_d1_a',
          date: shotTime.add(const Duration(hours: 6)),
          foodNoiseSuppression: 5,
          nauseaLevel: 2,
        ),
        DailySideEffectLog(
          id: 'se_d1_b',
          date: shotTime.add(const Duration(hours: 12)),
          foodNoiseSuppression: 3,
          nauseaLevel: 0,
        ),
        DailySideEffectLog(
          id: 'se_d2',
          date: shotTime.add(const Duration(hours: 30)),
          foodNoiseSuppression: 4,
          fatigueLevel: 4,
        ),
      ];

      final List<DayCorrelationProfile> profiles =
          FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine.calculate7DayProfile(
        sideEffectLogs: seLogs,
        injectionLogs: shots,
      );

      // Day 1 (index 0)
      expect(profiles[0].sampleCount, equals(2));
      expect(profiles[0].avgFoodNoiseSuppression, equals(4.0));
      expect(profiles[0].avgNausea, equals(1.0));

      // Day 2 (index 1)
      expect(profiles[1].sampleCount, equals(1));
      expect(profiles[1].avgFatigue, equals(4.0));
    });
  });

  group('Optimal Injection Timing Recommendation Tests', () {
    test('Peak symptom on Day 2 recommends Friday evening with bilingual rationale', () {
      final InjectionDayRecommendation recZh =
          FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine.recommendOptimalInjectionTiming(
        sideEffectLogs: <DailySideEffectLog>[],
        injectionLogs: <InjectionRecord>[],
        lang: AppLanguage.zhTW,
      );

      expect(recZh.peakSymptomDayOffset, equals(2));
      expect(recZh.recommendedWeekday, equals(DateTime.friday));
      expect(recZh.recommendedTimeLabel, contains('?曹???'));
      expect(recZh.rationale(AppLanguage.zhTW), contains('蝚?2 憭?));

      final InjectionDayRecommendation recEn =
          FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine.recommendOptimalInjectionTiming(
        sideEffectLogs: <DailySideEffectLog>[],
        injectionLogs: <InjectionRecord>[],
        lang: AppLanguage.enUS,
      );

      expect(recEn.recommendedTimeLabel, contains('Friday Evening'));
      expect(recEn.rationale(AppLanguage.enUS), contains('Day 2'));
    });

    test('Peak symptom on Day 3 dynamically shifts recommendation to Thursday evening', () {
      final DateTime shotTime = DateTime(2026, 11, 1, 8, 0);
      final List<InjectionRecord> shots = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'shot_d3',
          timestamp: shotTime,
          medication: Glp1Medication.semaglutide,
          doseMg: 1.0,
          site: InjectionSite.leftThigh,
        ),
      ];

      // ?賣?敺?54 撠? (?賣 Day 3, index 2) ?潛??湧??脣? (5??
      final List<DailySideEffectLog> seLogs = <DailySideEffectLog>[
        DailySideEffectLog(
          id: 'se_d3_peak',
          date: shotTime.add(const Duration(hours: 54)),
          foodNoiseSuppression: 5,
          fatigueLevel: 5,
          nauseaLevel: 4,
        ),
      ];

      final InjectionDayRecommendation rec =
          FoodNoiseCorrelationEngine.recommendOptimalInjectionTiming(
        sideEffectLogs: seLogs,
        injectionLogs: shots,
        lang: AppLanguage.zhTW,
      );

      expect(rec.peakSymptomDayOffset, equals(3));
      expect(rec.recommendedWeekday, equals(DateTime.thursday));
      expect(rec.recommendedTimeLabel, contains('?勗???'));
    });
  });
}


=== test/healthkit_sync_test.dart ===

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/healthkit_service.dart';

void main() {
  group('HealthKit Authorization & Sandbox Isolation Tests', () {
    test('HealthDataType identifiers match official Apple HealthKit constants', () {
      expect(
        HealthDataType.bodyMass.identifier,
        equals('HKQuantityTypeIdentifierBodyMass'),
      );
      expect(
        HealthDataType.dietaryProtein.identifier,
        equals('HKQuantityTypeIdentifierDietaryProtein'),
      );
      expect(
        HealthDataType.dietaryWater.identifier,
        equals('HKQuantityTypeIdentifierDietaryWater'),
      );
      expect(HealthDataType.bodyMass.defaultUnit, equals('kg'));
      expect(HealthDataType.dietaryProtein.defaultUnit, equals('g'));
      expect(HealthDataType.dietaryWater.defaultUnit, equals('mL'));
    });

    test('requestAuthorization operates safely under shielded sandbox without exceptions', () async {
      final HealthAuthStatus status =
          await HealthKitService.instance.requestAuthorization();
      expect(status, equals(HealthAuthStatus.authorized));
      expect(HealthKitService.instance.authStatus, equals(HealthAuthStatus.authorized));
    });
  });

  group('Bi-directional Sync & Micro-Habits Writing Tests', () {
    test('Weight bi-directional write and fetch operates accurately', () async {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final bool writeSuccess = await HealthKitService.instance
          .writeWeightSampleKg(82.4, now);
      expect(writeSuccess, isTrue);

      final double? latestWeight =
          await HealthKitService.instance.fetchLatestWeightKg();
      expect(latestWeight, isNotNull);
      expect(latestWeight, equals(82.4));
    });

    test('Protein and water cumulative tracking filters only today samples', () async {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final DateTime yesterday = now.subtract(const Duration(days: 1));

      // 1. 撖怠?冽?豢? (銝?閮隞蝝航?)
      await HealthKitService.instance.writeProteinSampleGrams(30, yesterday);
      await HealthKitService.instance.writeWaterSampleMl(500, yesterday);

      // 2. 撖怠隞憭活??豢?
      await HealthKitService.instance.writeProteinSampleGrams(25, now);
      await HealthKitService.instance.writeProteinSampleGrams(35, now);
      await HealthKitService.instance.writeWaterSampleMl(300, now);
      await HealthKitService.instance.writeWaterSampleMl(450, now);

      final int todayProtein =
          await HealthKitService.instance.fetchTodayProteinGrams();
      final int todayWater =
          await HealthKitService.instance.fetchTodayWaterMl();

      // 3. ?瑁?隞蝝航?蝎曄Ⅱ?寥?嚗??質釭 25 + 35 = 60g嚗ㄡ瘞?300 + 450 = 750mL
      expect(todayProtein, equals(60));
      expect(todayWater, equals(750));
    });
  });

  group('HealthSyncController Reactive State & Listener Tests', () {
    test('syncAll pulls latest data, updates cache, and toggles isSyncing cleanly', () async {
      final HealthSyncController controller = HealthSyncController.instance;

      int notifyCount = 0;
      void listener() => notifyCount++;
      controller.addListener(listener);

      try {
        await controller.syncAll();

        expect(controller.isSyncing, isFalse);
        expect(controller.lastSyncTime, isNotNull);
        expect(controller.authStatus, equals(HealthAuthStatus.authorized));
        expect(notifyCount, greaterThanOrEqualTo(2));
      } finally {
        controller.removeListener(listener);
      }
    });

    test('record methods update controller caches and notify UI listeners', () async {
      final HealthSyncController controller = HealthSyncController.instance;

      int listenerTriggered = 0;
      void listener() => listenerTriggered++;
      controller.addListener(listener);

      try {
        final bool weightLogged = await controller.recordWeight(79.5);
        expect(weightLogged, isTrue);
        expect(controller.latestSyncedWeightKg, equals(79.5));

        final int prevWater = controller.todaySyncedWaterMl;
        final bool waterLogged = await controller.recordWater(250);
        expect(waterLogged, isTrue);
        expect(controller.todaySyncedWaterMl, equals(prevWater + 250));

        final int prevProtein = controller.todaySyncedProteinGrams;
        final bool proteinLogged = await controller.recordProtein(20);
        expect(proteinLogged, isTrue);
        expect(controller.todaySyncedProteinGrams, equals(prevProtein + 20));

        expect(listenerTriggered, greaterThanOrEqualTo(3));
      } finally {
        controller.removeListener(listener);
      }
    });
  });
}


=== test/widget_test.dart ===

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/main.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/core/pharmacokinetics_engine.dart';
import 'package:dose_flow/features/calculator/syringe_calculator_view.dart';

void main() {
  group('Pharmacokinetics & Bateman PK Engine Tests', () {
    test('Tirzepatide single dose Bateman curve calculates valid non-negative values', () {
      final double c24 = PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
        doseMg: 2.5,
        medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
        elapsedHours: 24.0,
      );
      final double c36 = PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
        doseMg: 2.5,
        medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
        elapsedHours: 36.0,
      );
      final double c168 = PharmacokineticsEngine.singleDoseConcentrationAt(
        doseMg: 2.5,
        medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
        elapsedHours: 168.0,
      );

      // 撽?瞈漲憭扳?嗡?瘜Ｗ陸 (36h Tmax) 憭扳蝚?7 憭拇郭靚?(168h)
      expect(c24, greaterThan(0.0));
      expect(c36, greaterThan(0.0));
      expect(c168, greaterThan(0.0));
      expect(c36, greaterThan(c168));
    });

    test('recommendNextSite rotates through injection sites sequentially', () {
      final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
      final List<InjectionRecord> logs = <InjectionRecord>[
        InjectionRecord(
          id: 'test_1',
          timestamp: now,
          medication: Glp1Medication.tirzepatide,
          doseMg: 2.5,
          site: InjectionSite.leftUpperAbdomen,
        ),
      ];

      final InjectionSite nextSite =
          PharmacokineticsEngine.recommendNextSite(logs);
      expect(nextSite, equals(InjectionSite.leftLowerAbdomen));
    });
  });

  group('SyringeMathEngine & Poka-Yoke Unit Tests', () {
    test('Premixed mode: 5 mg/mL drawing 2.5 mg outputs exactly 50 Units (0.50 mL)', () {
      final SyringeCalculationResult result = SyringeMathEngine.calculate(
        mode: SyringeCalcMode.premixed,
        concentrationMgPerMl: 5.0,
        vialMassMg: 10.0,
        bacteriostaticWaterMl: 2.0,
        targetDoseMg: 2.5,
      );

      expect(result.isValid, isTrue);
      expect(result.status, equals(SyringeValidationStatus.valid));
      expect(result.volumeMl, equals(0.5));
      expect(result.u100Units, equals(50.0));
      expect(result.effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl, equals(5.0));
    });

    test('Reconstitution mode: 10 mg dry vial + 2 mL BAC water outputs 5.0 mg/mL', () {
      final SyringeCalculationResult result = SyringeMathEngine.calculate(
        mode: SyringeCalcMode.reconstitution,
        concentrationMgPerMl: 0.0,
        vialMassMg: 10.0,
        bacteriostaticWaterMl: 2.0,
        targetDoseMg: 2.5,
      );

      expect(result.isValid, isTrue);
      expect(result.effectiveConcentrationMgPerMl, equals(5.0));
      expect(result.u100Units, equals(50.0));
    });

    test('Over-capacity safety: drawing 15 mg from 5 mg/mL exceeds 100-Unit syringe', () {
      final SyringeCalculationResult result = SyringeMathEngine.calculate(
        mode: SyringeCalcMode.premixed,
        concentrationMgPerMl: 5.0,
        vialMassMg: 20.0,
        bacteriostaticWaterMl: 4.0,
        targetDoseMg: 15.0, // ?閬?3.0 mL = 300 Units
      );

      expect(result.isValid, isFalse);
      expect(result.status, equals(SyringeValidationStatus.exceedsSyringeCapacity));
      expect(result.u100Units, greaterThan(100.0));
    });

    test('Invalid input safety: zero or negative target dose is safely blocked', () {
      final SyringeCalculationResult result = SyringeMathEngine.calculate(
        mode: SyringeCalcMode.premixed,
        concentrationMgPerMl: 5.0,
        vialMassMg: 10.0,
        bacteriostaticWaterMl: 2.0,
        targetDoseMg: -2.5,
      );

      expect(result.isValid, isFalse);
      expect(result.status, equals(SyringeValidationStatus.invalidInput));
      expect(result.u100Units, equals(0.0));
    });
  });

  group('DoseFlowApp Widget Smoke & Bilingual Toggle Tests', () {
    testWidgets('DoseFlowApp launches with Onboarding, skips to MainShellScreen, and toggles bilingual language smoothly', (WidgetTester tester) async {
      // 1. 鋆? DoseFlowApp
      await tester.pumpWidget(const DoseFlowApp());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();

      // 2. 撽?擐活??甇?Ⅱ?? OnboardingView (??????蝚?1 甇交?憿?
      expect(find.text('?仿?'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('?函?迤?其蝙?典蝔?GLP-1 ??嚗?), findsOneWidget);

      // 3. 暺?????芷脣銝餌??MainShellScreen
      await tester.tap(find.text('?仿?'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();

      // 4. 撽??脣銝餌?Ｖ蒂皜脫??身蝜?銝剜?璅???閮????
      expect(find.text('?衣????Pro'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('?? 蝜葉'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('?? EN'), findsOneWidget);

      // 5. 暺?? LanguageTogglePill 銝剔??望?璅惜
      await tester.tap(find.text('?? EN'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();

      // 6. 撽?蝡?勗??蝢??望?璅???Tab
      expect(find.text('DoseFlow: GLP-1 Tracker'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Dashboard'), findsOneWidget);
    });
  });
}

=== .github/workflows/doseflow_ci.yml ===

name: DoseFlow Medical Compliance & CI/CD Pipeline

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "main", "release/*" ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ "main" ]

jobs:
  quality_gate_and_build:
    name: Quality Gate & Multi-Platform Build
    runs-on: macos-latest

    steps:
      - name: ? Checkout Repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: ?? Setup Flutter Environment
        uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
        with:
          channel: 'stable'
          cache: true
          cache-key: 'flutter-stable-macos'
          cache-path: '${{ runner.tool_cache }}/flutter'

      - name: ? Install Dependencies
        run: flutter pub get

      - name: ?? Quality Gate 1 - Static Analysis (Zero Warnings)
        run: flutter analyze

      - name: ?妒 Quality Gate 2 - Automated Unit & Widget Tests
        run: flutter test

      - name: ?? Build Android App Bundle (AAB)
        run: flutter build appbundle --release

      - name: ?? Build iOS App (No Codesign for CI check)
        run: flutter build ios --release --no-codesign

      - name: ? Upload Android Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: doseflow-android-release
          path: build/app/outputs/bundle/release/app-release.aab
          retention-days: 7
